A 1 changes the level (NRZ-M)	A 1 改变电平（NRZ-M）
A 2D smoothing of the waterfall.	对瀑布图做二维平滑。
A 70 cm Yagi or a good vertical is enough during a pass; pick the satellite, then start the receiver. Doppler is handled by the demodulator.	过境期间一根 70 厘米八木或良好的垂直天线即足够；选定卫星后启动接收机。多普勒由解调器处理。
A CSV with a radio ID column and callsign / name columns (radioid.net user.csv, or your own list).	带电台 ID 列和呼号/姓名列的 CSV（radioid.net 的 user.csv，或你自己的列表）。
A HackRF, Airspy or RTL-SDR needs WinUSB the same way.	HackRF、Airspy 或 RTL-SDR 同样需要 WinUSB 驱动。
A PNG, JPEG, BMP or other picture. It is scaled to the resolution below with the picture fit you choose.	PNG、JPEG、BMP 或其他图片。按你选择的适配方式缩放到下方分辨率。
A Sigfox device sends each message as one frame or three (ranks 1, 2 and 3) on different frequencies inside a 200 kHz channel, each about 100 Hz wide (100 bit/s D-BPSK; 600 bit/s in RC2 and RC4). A frame holds a frame type (it names the size and the rank), a length indicator, the downlink request flag, a 12 bit message counter, the 32 bit device ID, 0 to 12 payload bytes (or a single bit), 2 to 5 bytes of authentication code and a CRC-16. The second and third ranks are the same content under a convolution code.\n\nOnAir only receives. The CRC is checked; the authentication code needs the device's secret key and is never checked, and payloads are often encrypted by the application, so they are shown as hex.	Sigfox 设备将每条消息在一个 200 kHz 信道内的不同频率上发送一帧或三帧（等级 1、2、3），每帧约 100 Hz 宽（100 bit/s D-BPSK；RC2 和 RC4 为 600 bit/s）。一帧包含：帧类型（标明大小与等级）、长度指示、下行请求标志、12 位消息计数器、32 位设备 ID、0 至 12 字节有效载荷（或单比特）、2 至 5 字节认证码和 CRC-16。第二、三帧是相同内容经卷积码编码后的版本。\n\nOnAir 只接收。CRC 会校验；认证码需要设备密钥，永不校验，且有效载荷常被应用层加密，因此以十六进制显示。
A WAV audio file cannot be played as an IQ source.	WAV 音频文件不能作为 IQ 源播放。
A band with text running through it; direction and speed per block	一条文字穿行其中的横幅；每个文字块可设方向和速度
A bench for signals: nothing here is transmitted and no radio is used. To send a signal on air, use the Transmit panel (it stays behind Arm, your licence and the band plan).	信号试验台：此处内容不会被发射，也不使用电台。要空中发射信号，请使用「发射」面板（需先经「预备」、你的执照和频段规划）。
A blank canvas: add blocks (radio sources, spectrum, demodulators, audio, decoders, map, satellite tracker, rotator, transmitter ...) and arrange them as you like	空白画布：添加模块（电台源、频谱、解调器、音频、解码器、地图、卫星跟踪器、转台、发射机……）并随意布置
A blank workspace. Add block (or a right click here) puts a block on the canvas: start with a Radio source, then a Spectrum and waterfall, a Decoder, a Demodulator and an Audio output that use it. Drag a block by its title to move it, by a corner to resize it; drop it on another block's title to make tabs. The workspace list on the left has ready-made templates.	空白工作区。「添加模块」（或在此右键）可在画布上放置模块：先放一个「电台源」，再放使用它的「频谱与瀑布图」「解码器」「解调器」和「音频输出」。拖动模块标题可移动，拖动边角可缩放；把模块拖到另一个模块的标题上可合并为标签页。左侧的工作区列表有现成模板。
A callsign validated by EchoLink (echolink.org) is needed. The password is kept in the settings and never shown or logged.	需要经 EchoLink（echolink.org）验证的呼号。密码保存在设置中，绝不显示或记录。
A carrier must be this far above the noise floor to be found	载波必须高出底噪这么多才能被发现
A carrier, then a modulation that is not A2 Manchester (A3 QPSK, ZE, Argos-4?): not decoded, its specification is not public	一个载波，其后是 A2 曼彻斯特以外的调制（A3 QPSK、ZE、Argos-4？）：未解码，其规范未公开
A channel is listed once it was seen in two sweeps. Images of strong signals, steady spurs and the radio's DC spike are hidden (untick "confirmed channels only" to see them flagged).	频道在两次扫描中都出现后才会列出。强信号、稳定杂散和电台直流尖刺的虚像会被隐藏（取消勾选「仅已确认频道」可看到它们被标记）。
A channel scan has the radio	信道扫描占用电台时
A click on the spectrum or the waterfall tunes to a multiple of this step (Mode step: 100 kHz for FM, 12.5 kHz for PMR, else 1 kHz)	点击频谱或瀑布图将调谐到此步进的整数倍（模式步进：FM 为 100 kHz，PMR 为 12.5 kHz，其他为 1 kHz）
A click on the spectrum puts the radio centre there.	点击频谱将使电台中心置于该处。
A continuous 400 bit/s Manchester signal with made-up HDLC frames, following the Doppler curve of a pass.	连续的 400 bit/s 曼彻斯特信号，带虚构 HDLC 帧，随过境多普勒曲线变化。
A conversation ends after silence of (s)	静默（秒）持续多久后通话结束
A dipole (two rods in a V or a line)	偶极天线（两根振子成 V 形或一字排列）
A dipole is poor at this frequency.	偶极天线在此频率效果差。
A dipole works well when its rods match the band: about 75 cm each for FM, 53 cm for satellites at 137 MHz.	振子与波段匹配时偶极天线效果良好：FM 每根约 75 厘米，137 MHz 卫星约 53 厘米。
A discone (a cone of rods, usually outdoors)	盘锥天线（由多根振子组成的锥体，通常架设在室外）
A discone covers FM, airband, AIS and ADS-B. It is a good all-round choice.	盘锥天线覆盖 FM、航空频段、AIS 和 ADS-B，是良好的全能选择。
A discone is poor above 1.3 GHz.	盘锥天线在 1.3 GHz 以上效果差。
A discone is poor below 25 MHz.	盘锥天线在 25 MHz 以下效果差。
A discone is weak below 25 MHz: for FT8 add a long wire or a loop.	盘锥天线在 25 MHz 以下增益低：听 FT8 请加长导线天线或环形天线。
A drag moves the view, a click tunes	拖动平移视图，点击调谐
A file of the raw radio samples. Played back, it behaves like the radio at that moment, so any mode can decode it again.	原始无线电采样文件。回放时就像当时的电台，任何模式都能再次解码。
A file with errors is never imported. Fix the lines above and try again.	有错误的文件不会被导入。请修复上方各行后重试。
A fixed number of hertz added to what the radio is told, for a radio or converter that radiates a set amount off.	告知电台的频率上附加一个固定赫兹数，用于补偿有固定频偏的电台或变频器。
A frame that fails its check is tried again with one wrong bit (or two in a row) corrected. More frames from weak stations; a repaired frame is marked [1 bit fixed]. Off by default: a few repaired frames could be wrong.	校验失败的帧会尝试纠正一位（或连续两位）错误后重试。能收到更多弱台帧；修复的帧会标注 [修复 1 位]。默认关闭：少数被修复的帧可能有误。
A gate that keeps the sound muted until a signal is stronger than its level, so you do not hear hiss between transmissions.	门限：信号电平超过设定值前保持静音，这样在两段发射之间听不到噪声。
A grid is 2 letters, 2 digits and optionally 2 letters (KO24 / KO24pq).	网格定位器由 2 个字母、2 位数字和可选的 2 个字母组成（KO24 / KO24pq）。
A hit starts this far above the dynamic noise floor of its own channel.	命中须高出其自身信道的动态底噪这么多。
A host:port address needs model 2 (rigctld), not this serial rig.	host:port 地址需要型号 2（rigctld），而不是此串口电台。
A large level bar with peak hold, for pointing a dish.	带峰值保持的大电平条，用于对准天线。
A line at the noise floor of the view (the 20th percentile of the trace) with its level	在视图底噪处画一条线（迹线第 20 百分位）并标注其电平
A link sends what the radio receives to the EchoLink connection and transmits what EchoLink sends. Running a link or repeater node needs an amateur licence that allows it and, in many countries, a separate permit for unattended or remote operation. You are responsible for identifying the station, for staying in your licence's bands and for what is relayed. EchoLink also asks link nodes to be registered with -L or -R.	链路把电台收到的话音发给 EchoLink 连接，并把 EchoLink 发来的内容发射出去。运行链路或中继节点需要允许这样做的业余执照，在许多国家还需要无人值守或远程操作的单独许可。你有责任识别电台、遵守执照规定的频段，并对转发内容负责。EchoLink 还要求链路节点注册 -L 或 -R 后缀。
A list is a group of memories. Switched-off lists are hidden from the panel and the quick-tune bar.	列表是一组存储信道。关闭的列表会在面板和快速调谐栏中隐藏。
A lock means the receiver found and decoded the signal. A mode without a lock indicator in the engine shows no lock here; check its own panel (Open in decoder).	锁定表示接收机已找到并解码信号。引擎中没有锁定指示的模式此处不显示锁定；请查看该模式自己的面板（在解码器中打开）。
A logo from a PNG file (transparency kept), scaled into its box	来自 PNG 文件的标志（保留透明），缩放到其方框内
A long wire	长导线天线
A long wire is good for short wave and FT8.	长导线天线适合短波和 FT8。

A loop is good for long, medium and short wave.	环形天线适合长波、中波和短波。
A lower frame rate cap and a simpler aircraft map (no trails). For computers without a graphics driver (software OpenGL).	更低的帧率上限和更简单的飞机地图（无轨迹）。适用于没有显卡驱动的电脑（软件 OpenGL）。
A made-up signal with a known structure (the same generator as the evaluation): see what the lab finds, then compare with what was sent.	结构已知的虚构信号（与评估用同一发生器）：看实验室能发现什么，再与实际发送的内容对比。
A marker at the station position on every map, and the distance and bearing from it to the pointer and to the selected object. Nothing is drawn until a position is set.	在每张地图上于电台位置画标记，并显示它到指针和所选目标的距离与方位。设置位置之前不绘制任何内容。
A moved or resized block settles on the grid and against the edges of the others	移动或缩放后的模块会对齐网格并贴合其他模块的边缘
A network radio is limited by its link: keep the sample rate low if the sound breaks up.	网络电台受其链路限制：声音断续时请降低采样率。
A new carrier must stay on this long before it is decoded	新载波须持续这么久才会被解码
A new over starts after a pause of (s)	暂停（秒）持续多久后算新的一句
A notch at the recording's centre in front of the demodulator: the SDR's own DC spike / LO leakage does not reach the signal. The spectrum still shows the spike.	在解调器前对录音中心加陷波：SDR 自身的直流尖刺/本振泄漏不会进入信号。频谱上仍能看到该尖刺。
A notification and a sound, at most every 30 minutes while it holds. Edit or switch it off in View > Automations.	触发期间每 30 分钟至多提醒一次（通知+声音）。可在「视图 > 自动化」中编辑或关闭。
A password is set. It is stored only as a salted hash, never as text.	已设置密码。仅保存加盐哈希，绝不保存明文。
A path arrives beyond the guard interval: it interferes. A directional antenna away from it helps.	一条路径晚于保护间隔到达：会造成干扰。用定向天线避开该方向有帮助。
A playlist address is added to your lists and its channels shown on the right. A single stream (HLS .m3u8, MPEG-TS over http, udp:// or rtp://, also multicast) starts playing.	播放列表地址会加入你的列表，其频道显示在右侧。单个流（HLS .m3u8、http 上的 MPEG-TS、udp:// 或 rtp://，含组播）会直接开始播放。
A pulsar is thousands of times weaker than the noise in a single sample. It shows only after minutes to hours of folding, and only if the antenna collects enough: roughly one square metre of effective area with a low-noise amplifier at the feed (a dish of 1.5 to 2 m, or an array of several long Yagis) for the brightest northern pulsar B0329+54 at 408 MHz. A whip, a discone or a wideband dipole (0.1 m2) will not show any pulsar. The numbers come from the radiometer equation below and from simulated data; this mode has not been tested on a real pulsar.	脉冲星比单个采样中的噪声弱数千倍。只有经过数分钟到数小时的折叠才显现，且要求天线收集到足够能量：在北天最亮的脉冲星 B0329+54（408 MHz）上，馈源带低噪声放大器、约一平方米有效面积（1.5 至 2 米抛物面，或几副长八木组成的阵列）即可。鞭状天线、盘锥或宽带偶极（0.1 平方米）看不到任何脉冲星。数值来自下方辐射计方程和仿真数据；此模式尚未在真实脉冲星上测试过。
A quiet carrier keeps its decoder this long	静默载波让解码器保持这么久
A radio controlled over CAT (an Icom IC-R8600, an IC-7300, a rigctld on the network): OnAir and the rig follow each other's frequency and mode. IQ or audio comes from a sound card. OnAir never transmits through this.	通过 CAT 控制的电台（Icom IC-R8600、IC-7300、网络上的 rigctld）：OnAir 与电台互相同步频率和模式。IQ 或音频来自声卡。OnAir 从不通过它发射。
A radio on another computer or on the network: a PlutoSDR by IP, an rtl_tcp server, a SpyServer, an SDR++ server, SoapyRemote, a Hermes Lite 2 or a Red Pitaya	另一台电脑或网络上的电台：IP 连接的 PlutoSDR、rtl_tcp 服务器、SpyServer、SDR++ 服务器、SoapyRemote、Hermes Lite 2 或 Red Pitaya
A radio on the network is not found by looking: add it under Setup wizard or the source list.	网络上的电台无法通过搜索发现：请在「设置向导」或源列表中添加。
A radio with a GPSDO or a PPS input: pick that time source in the radio's settings; the samples then carry hardware time.	带 GPSDO 或 PPS 输入的电台：在该电台的设置中选择该时间源，采样即携带硬件时间。
A ratio on a log scale: +3 dB is double the power, +10 dB is ten times, +20 dB a hundred times.	对数刻度上的比值：+3 dB 是功率两倍，+10 dB 是十倍，+20 dB 是一百倍。
A receiver set to the wrong width finds nothing: leave it on Automatic unless the channel plan says otherwise.	带宽设置错误的接收机什么也找不到：除非信道规划另有要求，请保持「自动」。
A recording is the usual way to use this link	使用此链路通常靠录音
A regular 100 / 120 Hz impulse train (mains interference) is being ignored.	规则性的 100 / 120 Hz 脉冲串（市电干扰）正被忽略。
A repeat needs an end: turn off Repeat until stopped, or set the timeout timer.	重复发射需要一个终点：关闭「持续重复」，或设置超时定时器。
A sample this many times the mean level starts a blank: lower catches weaker clicks but may cut signal peaks	采样达到均值这么多倍时触发消隐：数值越低越能捕捉弱咔嗒声，但可能削掉信号峰值
A satellite is only heard while it passes overhead, a few minutes at a time. The Passes window lists when the next ones come, from your location.	卫星只在过顶时才能听到，每次几分钟。「过境」窗口按你的位置列出接下来的过境时刻。
A second sensor fills what the first does not send (for example a rain gauge next to a thermometer).	第二个传感器补充第一个不发送的数据（例如温度计旁的雨量计）。
A sensor that has not been heard for this long leaves the list (a car passing with tyre sensors, a neighbour's thermometer). Sensors you pin keep their place. Most sensors send every 30 to 180 seconds.	这么久没听到的传感器会离开列表（路过的胎压传感器、邻居的温度计）。你钉住的传感器会保留位置。多数传感器每 30 至 180 秒发送一次。
A serial GPS (macOS /dev/cu.usbmodem..., Linux /dev/ttyACM0 or /dev/ttyUSB0, 9600 baud; Windows \\\\.\\COM3) or an NMEA log file. Press Enter.	串口 GPS（macOS /dev/cu.usbmodem…、Linux /dev/ttyACM0 或 /dev/ttyUSB0，9600 波特；Windows \\\\.\\COM3）或 NMEA 日志文件。按回车确认。
A ship list or coast station list as CSV / TXT / TSV (for example from yaddnet.org, saved by you): MMSI, name, call sign, country, and for coast stations latitude and longitude, or a position in degrees and minutes. Delimiter and columns are found automatically; the list is merged into the local table.	船舶列表或海岸电台列表，CSV / TXT / TSV 格式（例如来自 yaddnet.org，由你保存）：MMSI、船名、呼号、国家，海岸电台还需纬度和经度，或以度分表示的位置。分隔符和列会自动识别；列表会合并到本地表中。
A short measurement with the present correction: does it still hold? Nothing is stored.	用当前修正做一次快速测量：是否仍然成立？不做任何保存。
A short telescopic or magnet whip. Fine for FM and strong local signals.	短拉杆天线或磁吸鞭状天线。适用于 FM 和较强的本地信号。
A short-wave loop is weak above 30 MHz: for VHF and UHF use a whip, a dipole or a discone.	短波环形天线在 30 MHz 以上增益低：VHF 和 UHF 请用鞭状、偶极或盘锥天线。
A site is where sondes are first heard low and climbing (within 5 km is the same site). A landing is the last position after a burst, below 1500 m.	发射场是探空仪首次在低空被听到且正在爬升的位置（5 公里内视为同一场）。着陆点是爆裂后最后的、低于 1500 米的位置。
A slow waterfall shows the average of the frames in between, not the highest level.	慢速瀑布图显示帧间平均值，而不是最高电平。
A small DC added to I. With Q, cancels the carrier the radio leaks at its centre: adjust for the smallest spike at the centre frequency.	在 I 路加入少量直流。配合 Q 路可抵消电台在中心泄漏的载波：调到中心频率尖刺最小为止。
A small DC added to Q (see Carrier leak I).	在 Q 路加入少量直流（见「载波泄漏 I」）。
A small signed list kept by the OnAir maintainers: listed, reviewed and verified plugins.	由 OnAir 维护者维护的小型签名列表：收录、审查并验证过的插件。

A source is https://host[/path], with no user name, query or spaces.	源地址格式为 https://主机[/路径]，不含用户名、查询串或空格。
A station's pilot is not always exact: repeat on two or three stations. Strong, steady stations are best.	某个电台的导频不一定精确：请在两三个电台上重复测量。信号强且稳定的电台最佳。
A step of more than the threshold within ten minutes on a station that is clearly present is marked. Sunrise and sunset along the path also change a signal (slowly); check a mark against the GOES X-ray flux before calling it a flare.	信号明显存在的情况下，十分钟内电平变化超过阈值即标记。日出日落沿线也会改变信号（缓慢）；宣布耀斑之前，请对照 GOES X 射线通量核实标记。
A test signal made inside OnAir (a TV programme, FM and more). You can try everything with it; recordings (IQ files) open from Source.	OnAir 内部生成的测试信号（电视节目、FM 等）。可以用它尝试一切功能；录音（IQ 文件）从「源」打开。

ATIS	ATIS
ATIS (ETSI EN 300 698-1 annex B) is sent by the radio of a ship on the European inland waterways when the operator lets go of the push-to-talk key: 1200 baud, 1300 / 2100 Hz tones in the DSC code. It is not AIS. The number is 9 + the country digits + the call sign (or 9 + the MMSI on radios programmed that way). The voice plays as narrow FM; the check symbol and the second copy of every symbol repair most errors.	ATIS（ETSI EN 300 698-1 附录 B）由欧洲内河航道船舶的电台在操作员松开 PTT 键时发送：1200 波特，DSC 码中的 1300 / 2100 Hz 音调。它不是 AIS。号码为 9 + 国家码 + 呼号（或按此编程的电台为 9 + MMSI）。话音以窄带 FM 播放；校验符号和每个符号的第二份拷贝可修复多数错误。

Amateur voice by the IARU band plan of your region: lower sideband below 10 MHz, upper sideband above (60 m upper), FM for the 10 m, 6 m, 2 m and 70 cm segments. A click tunes the segment's calling or middle frequency and sets the demodulator and filter. The segments are the IARU plans; your country's plan can be narrower. 30 m has no voice; 11 m is CB, not amateur. The region follows your locator (Ham setup, receiver position or setup location); choose it by hand under Advanced. See docs/ham-voice.md.	按你所在地区的 IARU 频段规划收听业余语音：10 MHz 以下用下边带，以上用上边带（60 米为上边带），10、6、2 米和 70 厘米分段用 FM。点击可调到该分段的呼叫频率或中间频率并设置解调器和滤波器。分段采用 IARU 规划；你所在国家的规划可能更窄。30 米没有语音；11 米是 CB，不是业余频段。区域跟随你的定位器（「业余设置」、接收位置或设置中的位置）；可在「高级」下手动选择。见 docs/ham-voice.md。

An IQ clip is the whole radio band from the start of the call: size = sample rate x 8 bytes x seconds (10 Msps x 10 s = 800 MB). It also contains neighbouring channels.	IQ 片段是呼叫开始时起的整个电台频段：大小 = 采样率 x 8 字节 x 秒数（10 Msps x 10 秒 = 800 MB）。其中也包含邻近信道。
An aircraft that has not been heard for this long leaves the table and the map. Never keeps them until the list is cleared (the table holds at most 2000).	这么久没听到的飞机会离开表格和地图。「永不」表示除非清空列表否则一直保留（表格最多 2000 条）。
An aircraft that was flying and now stands still on the ground more than 3 km from any airfield in the airport table: a possible out-landing (a retrieve, or a crew that may need help). Each landing is listed once.	飞机原本在飞，如今停在距机场表任一机场 3 公里以上的地面：可能是场外降落（需要回收，或机组可能需要帮助）。每次降落只列出一次。

Argentina	阿根廷
Argos collects data from small transmitters (PTTs) on buoys, floats, animals and weather stations through low-orbit satellites. A PTT sends a burst every minute or so on 401.650 MHz: 160 ms of plain carrier, then the message at 400 bit/s. In the A2 format the message holds 24 synchronisation bits, a 4 bit length field, the 28 bit platform ID and 24 to 248 bits of data. The satellite locates the PTT from the Doppler shift of its bursts. Newer formats (A3, ZE, Argos-4) use other modulations whose specifications are not public: OnAir lists those bursts without decoding them.\n\nThe satellites (MetOp-B, MetOp-C) also send a downlink on 465.9875 MHz: a 400 bit/s Manchester signal carrying HDLC frames, with a Doppler shift of up to about 10 kHz during a pass. What the frames hold is not public; they are shown as hex.\n\nOnAir only receives.	Argos 通过低轨卫星收集浮标、漂流浮标、动物和气象站上的小型发射机（PTT）数据。PTT 每分钟左右在 401.650 MHz 发送一次突发：160 毫秒纯载波，然后以 400 bit/s 发送消息。A2 格式中，消息包含 24 位同步位、4 位长度字段、28 位平台 ID 和 24 至 248 位数据。卫星根据突发的多普勒频移定位 PTT。较新的格式（A3、ZE、Argos-4）使用规范未公开的其他调制：OnAir 只列出这些突发，不解码。\n\n卫星（MetOp-B、MetOp-C）还在 465.9875 MHz 发送下行链路：400 bit/s 曼彻斯特信号，承载 HDLC 帧，过境时多普勒频移最高约 10 kHz。帧内容未公开，以十六进制显示。\n\nOnAir 只接收。

At AOS the receiver tunes, sets the mode, follows the Doppler shift, records and decodes, then saves. A receiver in another mode is switched for the pass and put back afterwards.	AOS 时接收机调谐、设置模式、跟踪多普勒、录音并解码，然后保存。其他模式下的接收机会为过境临时切换，结束后换回。
At high sample rates the receiver runs on a second CPU core, behind the front end, so a fast computer keeps up with more. Automatic: from 4 Msps on a computer with 4 or more cores, not in Low power mode. The results are the same; takes effect at the next Start.	高采样率下，接收机在前端之后的第二个 CPU 核心上运行，性能强的电脑能处理更多。自动：4 核及以上电脑从 4 Msps 起启用（低功耗模式除外）。结果相同；下次「开始」时生效。

BGAN	BGAN
BGAN bearers	BGAN 承载
BGAN is the Inmarsat data service. This lists its forward carriers (the satellite to terminal side) and reads the system information each one broadcasts: which satellite, which spot beam, which network, how busy the return channels are. It does not decode voice or data, does not show who is connected, and has only been tested on signals made by the test generator, never on a real BGAN recording.	BGAN 是 Inmarsat 的数据业务。此功能列出其前向载波（卫星到终端方向）并读取每个载波广播的系统信息：哪颗卫星、哪个点波束、哪个网络、返向信道有多忙。它不解码语音或数据，不显示谁已连接，且仅在测试发生器生成的信号上测试过，从未用真实 BGAN 录音测试。

Bundled (default): the FFmpeg inside OnAir. External folder: your own FFmpeg build (shared libavcodec, libavutil, e.g. an official build with software codecs) is used by the optional add-ons that load FFmpeg at run time (AC-4, AVS+). OnAir ships and downloads no extra codecs; the TV, DAB and DRM decoders keep using the bundled FFmpeg.	内置（默认）：OnAir 内的 FFmpeg。外部文件夹：你自己的 FFmpeg 构建（共享 libavcodec、libavutil，例如带软件编解码器的官方构建）供运行时加载 FFmpeg 的可选附加组件（AC-4、AVS+）使用。OnAir 不附带也不下载额外编解码器；电视、DAB 和 DRM 解码器继续使用内置 FFmpeg。

CBR	恒定码率（CBR）
CBR gives a predictable rate; VBR varies with the scene and needs headroom for the peaks	CBR 速率可预测；VBR 随场景变化，需为峰值预留余量
CBR: a constant rate, predictable, what a broadcast link needs. Constrained VBR: the rate varies with the scene up to 1.5 times the bitrate, so the mux rate needs headroom for the peaks. VBR / CRF: a constant quality instead of a rate (x264 and x265 only), capped at twice the bitrate.	CBR：恒定速率、可预测，是广播链路的需要。受限 VBR：速率随场景变化，最高为码率的 1.5 倍，因此复用速率需为峰值预留余量。VBR / CRF：恒定质量而非恒定速率（仅 x264 和 x265），上限为码率的两倍。
CCCH load: %llu blocks, %.0f%% paging, %.0f%% assignments, %.0f%% rejects, %.0f%% idle (message kinds only, no contents read)	CCCH 负载：%llu 个块，%.0f%% 寻呼，%.0f%% 指派，%.0f%% 拒绝，%.0f%% 空闲（仅消息类型，不读取内容）
CHANNELS	信道
CIS OFDM HDR modem (CIS-45, CIS-60, CIS-93): identification only, from the OFDM symbol, the carriers and the PSK order.	CIS OFDM HDR 调制解调器（CIS-45、CIS-60、CIS-93）：仅识别，依据 OFDM 符号、载波数和 PSK 阶数。
CIS-12 (MS5, AT-3004D, FIRE): 12 carriers 700 - 2900 Hz at 120 Bd with a 3300 Hz pilot. Physical layer only: the pilot, the PSK order and the phase steps of every carrier. No frame format is published.	CIS-12（MS5、AT-3004D、FIRE）：12 个载波，700 - 2900 Hz，120 波特，带 3300 Hz 导频。仅物理层：导频、PSK 阶数和每个载波的相位步进。未公开帧格式。
CIS-128: identification only: about 128 OFDM carriers at about 21 Bd in 3 kHz (the 6 kHz form is not covered).	CIS-128：仅识别：3 kHz 内约 128 个 OFDM 载波，约 21 波特（不含 6 kHz 形式）。
CIS-14 (AMOR): 2-FSK at 96 Bd. The 14-bit frame is found from its period and its fields are shown raw (state bits, two 5-bit words, two parity bits); the parity rule is not published, so it is not checked.	CIS-14（AMOR）：96 波特 2-FSK。按周期找出 14 位帧，字段原始显示（状态位、两个 5 位字、两个校验位）；校验规则未公开，因此不做校验。
CIS-3000: identification only: a single 8-PSK carrier at 3000 Bd.	CIS-3000：仅识别：单个 8-PSK 载波，3000 波特。
CIS-36 (MFSK-36): identification only, from the tone count, the 40 Hz spacing and the 10 / 20 / 40 Bd tone rate.	CIS-36（MFSK-36）：仅识别，依据音调数、40 Hz 间隔和 10 / 20 / 40 波特音调速率。
CIS-36-50 and CIS-50-50 (BEE, T-600): 2-FSK, 36 / 50 Bd idle and 50 / 100 Bd traffic, found automatically. The start word, the session key and the 3:4 characters are shown as hex: the traffic is encrypted and nothing is decrypted.	CIS-36-50 和 CIS-50-50（BEE、T-600）：2-FSK，空闲 36 / 50 波特，业务 50 / 100 波特，自动识别。起始字、会话密钥和 3:4 字符以十六进制显示：业务已加密，不做任何解密。

DVB-SH (satellite and terrestrial mobile TV, S band 2170 - 2200 MHz, also UHF and L band): the OFDM form (SH-A) and the TDM satellite form (SH-B) with the 3GPP2 turbo code and the long time interleaver. Written from ETSI EN 302 583 and tried only on generated test signals.	DVB-SH（卫星与地面移动电视，S 波段 2170 - 2200 MHz，也有 UHF 和 L 波段）：OFDM 形式（SH-A）和 TDM 卫星形式（SH-B），采用 3GPP2 turbo 码和长时交织。依据 ETSI EN 302 583 编写，仅在生成信号上测试。
DVB-SH OFDM (SH-A): 1K, 2K, 4K or 8K carriers, QPSK or 16-QAM (also hierarchical), the 3GPP2 turbo code at rates 1/5 to 2/3, a bit interleaver and a 48-branch time interleaver that can spread each codeword over seconds. The receiver finds the FFT size and guard from the cyclic prefix, the carrier offset from the pilots, the code rate and interleaver from the TPS, and checks the CRC-16 of every packet.	DVB-SH OFDM（SH-A）：1K、2K、4K 或 8K 载波，QPSK 或 16-QAM（也支持分层），1/5 至 2/3 码率的 3GPP2 turbo 码，比特交织器和可将每个码字分散到数秒的 48 分支时间交织器。接收机从循环前缀找出 FFT 大小和保护间隔，从导频找出载波偏移，从 TPS 找出码率和交织器，并校验每个数据包的 CRC-16。

Downloads the public SatNOGS DB lists (db.satnogs.org, read only, nothing is sent). Cached for 24 hours.	下载公开的 SatNOGS DB 列表（db.satnogs.org，只读，不发送任何内容）。缓存 24 小时。
Downloads the repeaters within 300 km of your position from openrepeater.org (CC0 data) with your own API key (free account, 30 requests a day), and keeps a copy on this computer. Needs your position (View > Map) and the key below.	用你自己的 API 密钥（免费账户，每天 30 次请求）从 openrepeater.org（CC0 数据）下载你位置 300 公里内的中继台，并在本机保存一份。需要你的位置（「视图 > 地图」）和下方密钥。
Drag a block from this list onto the picture, or double-click it to put it in the middle. Drag a block to move it, and the blue corner to resize a logo or the rolling band. Magnetic: blocks snap to each other's edges and to the picture's edges and centre; hold Alt to place freely. Click a block to change its text, font size, colour and box on the right; Delete removes it. Parameter blocks show the current value from the first tab and follow it. What you see is drawn by the same code as the encoded picture; while encoding, the picture here is the encoder's own and your changes go out on the next frame. More in docs/VIDEO-GENERATOR.md.	从列表把模块拖到图像上，或双击放到中央。拖动模块可移动，拖动蓝色角可缩放标志或滚动横幅。磁性吸附：模块对齐彼此边缘及图像边缘和中心；按住 Alt 自由放置。点击模块可在右侧面板修改文字、字号、颜色和方框；Delete 删除。参数模块显示第一个选项卡的当前值并跟随其变化。你看到的画面与编码画面由同一代码绘制；编码过程中，这里的画面属于编码器本身，你的更改会在下一帧发出。详见 docs/VIDEO-GENERATOR.md。

Encodes a camera, a still picture or a video file, with the overlay of the second tab, into an MPEG transport stream: a .ts file, or a UDP / RTP stream to watch in VLC. A .ts file can be given to the transmitter as its "MPEG-TS / video file". The estimate on the right of the first tab shows the bitrate the settings need, so you can fit the stream to your DVB link before encoding; the tab Digital Television Mode Selector shows which DVB modes carry it in your bandwidth at your C/N. Each setting has its own (?). The whole guide: docs/VIDEO-GENERATOR.md.	把摄像头、静态图片或视频文件连同第二选项卡的叠加层编码成 MPEG 传输流：.ts 文件，或供 VLC 观看的 UDP / RTP 流。.ts 文件可以作为「MPEG-TS / 视频文件」交给发射机。第一个选项卡右侧的估算显示了当前设置所需的码率，方便你在编码前把码流适配到 DVB 链路；「数字电视模式选择器」选项卡显示你的带宽和 C/N 下有哪些 DVB 模式能承载它。每项设置都有自己的 (?）。完整指南：docs/VIDEO-GENERATOR.md。

Enhance	增强
Enhanced ShockBurst frames of nRF24L01(+) chips (wireless mice and keyboards, sensors, toys, RC links). The address is not on the air as such: it is found by trying 3 to 5 byte addresses and accepting a frame whose CRC is valid. A frame with a CRC-8 (less certain than a CRC-16 one) is shown once it is seen three times. Frames of other 2.4 GHz systems (Bluetooth LE, Wi-Fi, Zigbee) are not ESB and give no frame.	nRF24L01(+) 芯片的增强型 ShockBurst 帧（无线鼠标键盘、传感器、玩具、遥控链路）。地址并不直接在空中传送：通过尝试 3 至 5 字节地址并接受 CRC 有效的帧来找到。CRC-8 帧（确定性低于 CRC-16）在被看到三次后显示。其他 2.4 GHz 系统（蓝牙 LE、Wi-Fi、Zigbee）的帧不是 ESB，得不到帧。

Every IP packet (DVB-H MPE, DVB-C2 GSE) goes to broadcast_ip.pcap in the data folder (libpcap, link type RAW). Open it in Wireshark. Switching it on again starts a new file.	每个 IP 数据包（DVB-H MPE、DVB-C2 GSE）写入数据文件夹的 broadcast_ip.pcap（libpcap，链路类型 RAW）。用 Wireshark 打开。再次开启时会新建文件。
Every QSO logged gets the contest id and the exchange; the serial number counts up; dupes are marked in the decode list.	记录的每个 QSO 都会带上竞赛标识和交换信息；序列号递增；重复通联在解码列表中标记。
Every call and sounding heard is a measurement of the path from that station on that frequency at that time of day. The best channel to reach each station now is worked out from those, from what was heard in the last half hour, and from the HF propagation monitor.	听到的每次呼叫和探测，都是该电台该频率该时段的传播路径测量。现在联系每个电台的最佳信道由这些测量、最近半小时的收听情况和 HF 传播监测共同推算。

Every message OnAir decodes, in every mode, is appended to a file in the folder below: one file per day per mode (for example onair-Inmarsat-C-2026-10-31.jsonl). Extra large	特大
Extra options for the video encoder, written like on the ffmpeg command line: -preset slow -tune zerolatency -x264-params keyint=50. They are passed to the encoder as a list of options, never through a shell, and they win over the settings above. Options that would change the input, output, container, codec, filters or hardware set-up (-i, -f, -c, -vf, -map, -hwaccel ...) are refused. An option the encoder does not know is reported under the picture after starting. Audio options are not taken.	视频编码器的额外选项，写法与 ffmpeg 命令行相同：-preset slow -tune zerolatency -x264-params keyint=50。它们以选项列表传给编码器，绝不经过 shell，且优先于上方设置。会更改输入、输出、容器、编解码器、滤波器或硬件设置的选项（-i、-f、-c、-vf、-map、-hwaccel …）会被拒绝。编码器不认识的选项会在启动后显示在图像下方。音频选项不生效。
Extra text scale of the touch interface.	触控界面的额外文字缩放。

FM de-emphasis	FM 去加重
FM radios send pre-emphasised audio (6 dB per octave, 750 us); this takes it out. Off gives flat audio.	FM 电台发送预加重音频（每倍频程 6 dB，750 微秒）；此项将其去除。关闭则为平坦音频。
FM squelch (dB SNR)	FM 静噪（dB SNR）
FM stations are between 87.5 and 108 MHz in most places. In FM mode, tune to a strong stripe; the station name and song appear when the station sends RDS.	多数地区 FM 电台在 87.5 至 108 MHz 之间。在 FM 模式下调到一个强条纹；电台发送 RDS 时会显示台名和歌曲。
FM stereo	FM 立体声
FM: force mono	FM：强制单声道

FT8 / FT4 / WSPR	FT8 / FT4 / WSPR
FT8 CQ	FT8 CQ
FT8 and other amateur data modes on the short-wave bands.	FT8 及其他短波业余数据模式。
FT8 and other amateur radio data modes on the short-wave bands.	FT8 及其他短波业余无线电数据模式。
FT8 basics	FT8 基础
FT8 is a ham radio mode for weak signals: short messages with call signs, signal reports and locators, every 15 seconds. On 20 metres it is at 14.074 MHz. It needs a short-wave antenna, and an upconverter for radios that start at 24 MHz.	FT8 是一种弱信号业余模式：每 15 秒发送含呼号、信号报告和网格定位器的短消息。20 米波段位于 14.074 MHz。需要短波天线；起始频率为 24 MHz 的电台还需上变频器。

Finds a steady whistle and cuts it, following it as it drifts (an adaptive line enhancer).	找到稳定的啸叫并切除，随其漂移持续跟踪（自适应谱线增强器）。
Finds coordinates (decimal, degrees and minutes, UTM, MGRS) and place names from pager_places.csv (name,lat,lon per line) in the data folder, names the parts each address always sends, and groups pages of several addresses about one event. All of it stays on this computer.	从数据文件夹的 pager_places.csv（每行 name,lat,lon）查找坐标（十进制度、度分、UTM、MGRS）和地名，为每个地址常发的部分命名，并把多个地址关于同一事件的页面分组。所有内容都留在本机。
Finds every LoRa signal in the whole capture at once, whatever its spreading factor, bandwidth or sync word, and says which network it looks like (Meshtastic preset, LoRaWAN plan, LoRa APRS, MeshCore, private). Nothing has to be set first.	一次性找到整个采集中的所有 LoRa 信号，无论扩频因子、带宽或同步字，并判断它像哪个网络（Meshtastic 预设、LoRaWAN 规划、LoRa APRS、MeshCore、私有）。无需任何预设。
Finds other OnAir programs on this local network and lists what they are doing. Off until you switch it on. Stays on this network (nothing goes to the internet) and sends no IQ and no audio. Controlling another node needs a one-time pairing; transmit and the voice keys can never be reached.	在本地网络上查找其他 OnAir 程序并列出它们正在做什么。默认关闭。只在本网络内（不发送任何内容到互联网），不发送 IQ 和音频。控制其他节点需要一次性配对；发射和语音密钥永远无法被触及。
Finds the darkest vertical bar and moves it to the left edge.	找到最暗的竖直条并移到左边缘。
Finds the modulation and symbol rate of the selection and fills them in. You can change them afterwards.	查找选区的调制方式和符号速率并填入。之后可以修改。
Finds the speed, shift and polarity from the signal and follows it when it changes. Needs about 6 s of signal.	从信号中查找速率、频移和极性并随其变化跟踪。需要约 6 秒信号。

Finds wireless microphone transmitters: wide FM carriers (about 40 to 200 kHz) in the UHF bands of your region, and narrow FM ones (25 kHz channels) in the VHF bands 169 - 171, 173.8 - 175.0 and 174 - 216 MHz. Pick a band to tune the radio to it. Click a candidate to listen. Many systems compress the audio (companding), which sounds harsh and breathing without the matching expander; digital systems show as wide carriers that are not FM.	查找无线麦克风发射机：你所在地区的 UHF 频段宽带 FM 载波（约 40 至 200 kHz），以及 VHF 频段 169 - 171、173.8 - 175.0 和 174 - 216 MHz 的窄带 FM（25 kHz 信道）。选择频段把电台调过去，点击候选者收听。许多系统使用音频压缩（压扩），没有匹配的扩展器时听感生硬、带喘息声；数字系统显示为非 FM 的宽带载波。
Finer columns keep narrow signals sharp when zoomed in (NFM, SSB, DMR, TETRA). Up to 64k columns (32 MB); never more than the FFT has bins.	更细的列在放大时保持窄信号清晰（NFM、SSB、DMR、TETRA）。最多 64k 列（32 MB）；从不超过 FFT 点数。

For ADS-B, a 1090 MHz antenna (6.9 cm vertical) high up and near a window sees planes much further away.	ADS-B 使用 1090 MHz 天线（6.9 厘米垂直振子），架高并靠近窗户能看远得多。
For TV, a roof or loft TV antenna pointed at the transmitter works far better than the whip.	看电视用指向发射台的屋顶或阁楼电视天线，效果远胜鞭状天线。
For VHF and UHF a long wire is a poor match: use a whip, a dipole or a discone.	VHF 和 UHF 用长导线匹配不佳：请用鞭状、偶极或盘锥天线。
For a computer whose UDP ports cannot be reached: everything goes through one TCP connection to an EchoLink proxy.	UDP 端口无法通达的电脑：所有流量经一条 TCP 连接走 EchoLink 代理。
For airband, put the whip near a window, pull it out to about 60 cm, or try a discone or a vertical outdoors.	听航空频段，把鞭状天线放到窗边、拉出约 60 厘米，或试试室外的盘锥或垂直天线。
For an amplifier, transverter or sequencer behind the SDR: the rig's PTT (CAT, RTS, DTR or VOX, as set up for the rig in the Ham view) is keyed just before the RF starts and released right after it stops. If the rig does not answer, the transmission goes on and a note says so.	用于 SDR 后级的放大器、变频板或时序器：电台的 PTT（CAT、RTS、DTR 或 VOX，按「业余」视图中该电台的设置）在射频开始前触发、结束后立即释放。若电台无响应，发射继续并给出提示。
For one narrow radio: at the start of each 2-minute slot the dial moves to the next band switched on for this UTC hour. The radio follows when it is under OnAir's control.	单部窄带电台：每 2 分钟时隙开始时，拨号移到该 UTC 小时内开启的下一个波段。电台在 OnAir 控制下会跟随。
For the layers the ascent did not measure (above the highest point heard, or all of them when the ascent was missed).\nOpen-Meteo needs the download switch in "me / prediction"; the SondeHub prediction needs the online flight prediction there.	用于上升段未测量的层次（高于听到的最高点，或漏收时全部层次）。\nOpen-Meteo 需要「我 / 预测」中的下载开关打开；SondeHub 预测需要那里的在线飞行预测。
For weather satellites, a V-dipole (two 53 cm rods at 120 degrees, lying flat) works well outdoors.	接收气象卫星，V 形偶极（两根 53 厘米振子成 120 度，平放）在室外效果很好。

Gain dB	增益 dB
Gain is how much the radio amplifies before it measures the signal. Too little and weak signals vanish in the noise; too much and strong ones distort everything.\nThe easy way: turn on automatic gain, which finds a good level for this mode.	增益是电台在测量信号前的放大倍数。太小，弱信号淹没在噪声中；太大，强信号把一切弄得失真。\n简单的办法：打开自动增益，它会为该模式找到合适的电平。

H.264 is widely supported and the safe choice for DVB-T. H.265 needs about 30 % less bitrate, but only newer receivers (DVB-T2 sets) decode it: check your whole chain. MPEG-2 works everywhere but needs about twice the bitrate of H.264. Only the encoders that work on this computer are listed. The FFmpeg in the Linux and macOS packages has MPEG-2 only; on Windows H.264 and H.265 come from Media Foundation (the graphics card when it has an encoder). An orange (!) next to the list tells which hardware encoders are missing here and why.	H.264 支持广泛，是 DVB-T 的稳妥选择。H.265 可省约 30 % less 码率，但只有较新的接收机（DVB-T2 电视机）能解码：请确认整条链路。MPEG-2 到处可用，但需要约两倍于 H.264 的码率。仅列出本机可用的编码器。Linux 和 macOS 包中的 FFmpeg 仅有 MPEG-2；Windows 上 H.264 和 H.265 来自 Media Foundation（显卡带编码器时使用）。列表旁的橙色 (!) 说明此处缺哪些硬件编码器及原因。
H.264 only. Baseline: simple decoders, no B-frames. Main: the broadcast default, every receiver decodes it. High: better compression, for newer receivers.	仅 H.264。Baseline：简单解码器，无 B 帧。Main：广播默认值，所有接收机都能解码。High：压缩更好，适合较新的接收机。

How far a crackle must stand over the noise floor to count. Lower finds weaker (more distant) strikes but also counts noise.	杂音必须高出底噪多少才算数。调低可发现更弱（更远）的闪电，但也会把噪声计入。
How far a signal stands above the noise floor, in decibels (dB). Around 10 dB is usable; 20 dB or more is clean.	信号高出底噪多少，以分贝（dB）计。约 10 dB 可用；20 dB 以上很干净。
How far the decoder got:	解码器进展到：
How far the listening frequency is from the radio's centre frequency	收听频率与电台中心频率的距离
How far the wanted signal is from the radio's centre frequency. OnAir often tunes a little off centre to avoid the spike at the centre.	所需信号与电台中心频率的距离。OnAir 经常稍微偏离中心调谐，以避开中心处的尖刺。
How far under the noise floor the colours start: more lifts the noise into brighter colours (the floor is followed over about 2 s; double click: the look's value)	颜色从底噪下方多少开始：越大越多噪声被抬入亮色（底噪约 2 秒内跟随；双击：外观的值）
How fast the max-hold line falls. 0 keeps it until reset.	最大保持线衰减多快。0 表示保持到重置。
How information rides on the radio wave: AM changes its strength, FM changes its frequency, digital modes switch between set states.	信息如何搭载在无线电波上：AM 改变波的强度，FM 改变波的频率，数字模式在设定的状态间切换。
How long each blank lasts after a click: as short as removes the click	每次咔嗒后消隐持续多久：以刚好消除咔嗒为准
How long the .ts file is. One minute for a still picture unless you change it; the file's own length for a video. A stream has no length: it runs until you press Stop.	.ts 文件的长度。静态图片默认一分钟（可改）；视频为文件自身长度。流没有长度：一直运行到你按「停止」。
How many audio tracks, each at the audio bitrate below.	音轨数量，每条使用下方的音频码率。
How many balloons are followed at once (1: the dial channel only).	同时跟踪多少个气球（1：仅拨号信道）。
How many dB the colours span from their start: a signal this far over the start is at the hot end (double click: the look's value)	颜色从起点跨越多少 dB：超出起点此值的信号位于热端（双击：外观的值）
How many frames (200) and stations (300) are kept before the oldest leave. Larger lists suit a full day of monitoring; they use more memory.	保留多少帧（200）和电台（300），超出的最旧记录被淘汰。更大的列表适合全天监测；占用更多内存。
How many measurements per second the radio sends. It sets how wide a piece of the spectrum you see at once: 2 million samples a second shows about 2 MHz.	电台每秒发送多少测量值。它决定你一次看到的频谱宽度：每秒 200 万个采样约显示 2 MHz。
How many rows of the history stand in the landscape (beyond 512 they build up while the view is open)	横向放置多少行历史（超过 512 时会在视图打开期间累积）
How many rows the lists keep before the oldest leave: standard is 100 packets, 150 nodes, 100 messages, 150 APRS stations, 200 LoRaWAN devices. Larger sizes use more memory and a little more CPU; Save results exports what is kept.	列表保留多少行，超出的最旧记录被淘汰：标准为 100 个数据包、150 个节点、100 条消息、150 个 APRS 电台、200 个 LoRaWAN 设备。更大的尺寸占用更多内存和稍多 CPU；「保存结果」导出当前保留的内容。
How many transmissions the schedule makes, the first included. 0: until Stop is pressed.	定时计划发射多少次（含第一次）。0：直到按下「停止」。
How much long transforms share with the one before. Auto keeps about 30 rows a second and limits 512k and 1M to 3/4. Less overlap: fewer rows a second and less CPU.	长变换与前一个共享多少。自动保持约每秒 30 行，并将 512k 和 1M 限制为 3/4。重叠越少：每秒行数越少，CPU 占用越低。
How much spectrum the radio captures around the frequency. The standard bandwidth is the mode's own width; tick Custom to set any width you like (a decoder that searches for carriers then finds every one inside it). A wider capture needs a higher sample rate and more processor time; a limit here only warns.	电台在频率周围采集多宽的频谱。标准带宽为模式自身的宽度；勾选「自定义」可设置任意宽度（搜索载波的解码器会找到其中每一个）。更宽的采集需要更高采样率和更多处理器时间；这里的限制仅作警告。
How much the radio amplifies before it measures. Too low: weak signals disappear. Too high: strong signals overload the radio and create false copies.	电台在测量前放大多少。太低：弱信号消失。太高：强信号使电台过载并产生假镜像。
How often the live ground-station list is asked for while it is used (2 minutes at the least).	使用实时地面站列表时多久请求一次（至少 2 分钟）。
How quickly Auto reacts (hold and settle times are divided by it). Overloads are always acted on at once.	自动增益反应多快（保持和稳定时间被它除）。过载总是立即处理。
How the channel numbers are counted: NESDIS (GOES), EUMETSAT TD 16 table 11 (Meteosat), or none for Himawari (JMA publishes frequencies only). Auto uses GOES below 402.0 MHz and Meteosat from there up.	信道编号的计算方式：NESDIS（GOES）、EUMETSAT TD 16 表 11（Meteosat），Himawari 无（JMA 仅公布频率）。自动在 402.0 MHz 以下用 GOES，以上用 Meteosat。
How the map and the table show the aircraft: altitude colours, track width, and when to drop an aircraft that is no longer heard	地图和表格如何显示飞机：高度配色、轨迹宽度，以及多久丢弃不再听到的飞机
How the radio's channel is turned into audio. SELCAL on the aeronautical HF channels (for example 8.879 MHz) is sent in USB: tune the dial frequency. FM for VHF / UHF radios, AM for the VHF airband.	电台信道如何变成声音。航空 HF 信道上的 SELCAL（例如 8.879 MHz）以 USB 发送：调到拨号频率。VHF / UHF 电台用 FM，VHF 航空频段用 AM。
How the tuned band is drawn over the spectrum and the waterfall.	调谐的频段如何叠加绘制在频谱和瀑布图上。
How thick the spectrum line is drawn.	频谱线画多粗。

Hytera privacy is not an ETSI standard: the method follows the open-source DSD-FME. Enter your own key. Letterbox	信箱模式
Letters, digits, accented Latin, Cyrillic and Greek are built in. For Chinese, Japanese, Korean or symbols choose a .ttf or .otf font file that has them (for example GNU Unifont or Noto Sans CJK, downloaded from their own sites; OnAir does not ship it). Characters the font lacks stay '?'.	字母、数字、带重音的拉丁文、西里尔文和希腊文已内置。中文、日文、韩文或符号请选择包含它们的 .ttf 或 .otf 字体文件（例如 GNU Unifont 或 Noto Sans CJK，从各自官网下载；OnAir 不附带）。字体缺少的字符显示为「?」。
Level of each tone (%)	每个音调的电平（%）
Level readout at the cursor	光标处电平读数
Level spread	电平分布
Levels	电平
Levels as dBm where the radio is calibrated.	按电台校准位置以 dBm 显示电平。
Levels as power per hertz: a bigger FFT splits the noise into narrower bins, which per bin reads lower; per Hz the floor stays where it is. Off: per FFT bin, as before.	按每赫兹功率显示电平：更大的 FFT 把噪声分成更窄的频点，每个频点读数更低；按 Hz 看底噪不变。关闭：按 FFT 频点显示，同以前。
Levels in dB per Hz	按 dB/Hz 显示电平
Libraries OnAir can use when you add them. Put the file in the codecs folder; it survives updates. Get each one only from its official page below: a DLL from an unofficial site can be malware.	OnAir 添加后可以使用的库。把文件放进 codecs 文件夹；可经受更新。请只从下方官方页面获取：非官方站点的 DLL 可能是恶意软件。

Light aircraft, 868 MHz	轻型飞机，868 MHz
Light or high contrast	浅色或高对比
Lightning map, several stations	闪电地图，多站
Lightning shows as short radio crackles (sferics) on every band below about 30 MHz, strongest at VLF / LF and in the evening on HF. Tune a quiet frequency (3 to 8 MHz, or an unused AM broadcast channel) and watch the strikes. One receiver gives the time and strength of each strike, not its position. Receive only.	闪电在约 30 MHz 以下每个频段都表现为短暂的无线电杂音（sferics），在 VLF / LF 最强，HF 傍晚最强。调到安静的频率（3 至 8 MHz，或未使用的 AM 广播信道）观察闪电。单台接收机给出每次闪电的时间和强度，而不是位置。仅接收。
Lightning, below 30 MHz	闪电，30 MHz 以下

Like !1a2b3c4d (8 hex digits). Empty: a stable number is made from your callsign, or the long name.	例如 !1a2b3c4d（8 个十六进制位）。留空：根据你的呼号或长名生成一个稳定的号码。

Links	链接
Links that are only a statistical guess, for example a deeper copy of a flooded packet following a shallower relay. They are never used for the single-point-of-failure check unless this is on.	仅是统计猜测的链路，例如泛洪数据包在深一层中继之后跟随的更深副本。除非开启此项，它们从不用于单点故障检查。
Linux: your account needs permission to open the radio. The udev rules (install-udev-rules.sh, done by the .deb) give it; unplug and replug afterwards. If the kernel's TV driver grabs an RTL-SDR, the rules blacklist it.	Linux：你的账户需要打开电台的权限。udev 规则（install-udev-rules.sh，.deb 已执行）会赋予权限；之后拔插电台。如果内核的电视驱动占用了 RTL-SDR，规则会将其列入黑名单。

Listening: a satellite is identified after a few ring alerts spread over 20 seconds or more.	收听中：卫星在数个分散于 20 秒以上的振铃告警后被识别。

Listens 20 s on each known pager frequency of your region and stops where a page is decoded.	在你所在地区的每个已知寻呼频率上监听 20 秒，解码到寻呼即停。
Listens 30 s on each band used in your region, settles where devices are heard and keeps sampling the others. One radio can still miss a device that sends rarely. Tuning by hand turns it off.	在你所在地区使用的每个频段上监听 30 秒，听到设备后驻留并继续扫描其他频段。发送稀疏的设备仍可能被单部电台漏掉。手动调谐会关闭它。
Listens with every sub-mode and period at once and shows what decodes; a wrong one fails its check. Uses more CPU than one setting.	同时以所有子模式和周期监听并显示解码结果；错误的设置会校验失败。比单一设置更耗 CPU。

Load capcode names from a CSV file: columns capcode (or a range like 1000-1099), name, category, protocol (POCSAG, FLEX or empty for both). Semicolon or tab separated files work too. Names with the same capcode and protocol are replaced, the rest stay.	从 CSV 文件加载 capcode 名称：列为 capcode（或 1000-1099 这样的范围）、名称、类别、协议（POCSAG、FLEX 或留空表示两者）。分号或制表符分隔的文件也可以。相同 capcode 和协议的名称会被替换，其余保留。

Looks a few seconds at each short wave band for channels shaped like DRM and lists them. A radio that captures at least 500 kHz is needed. The shape is a guess until it decodes.	依次查看每个短波频段几秒，列出形状像 DRM 的信道。需要至少能采集 500 kHz 的电台。解码成功前形状只是猜测。

Low-power drawing lowers the frame rate and simplifies the aircraft map (no trails, labels only for the selected aircraft). Installing the graphics driver for your GPU is the real fix.	低功耗绘制会降低帧率并简化飞机地图（无航迹，仅显示所选飞机的标签）。为显卡安装图形驱动才是真正的解决办法。

Lower is better quality and more bits. The rate is capped at twice the bitrate above.	越低画质越好、码率越高。速率上限为上方码率的两倍。

MORE  receiver details, history, memories, second mode	更多　接收机详情、历史、存储、第二模式
MPEG-2 tells the receiver with each picture how long to wait for it, at most about 0.7 s. Even at the lowest quality, a picture of this size needs about 160 bit/s per 16x16 block to arrive in time; below that the encoder cannot make a valid stream. For a lower bitrate, choose a smaller picture, or H.264 / H.265 if this FFmpeg has them.	MPEG-2 随每幅图像告知接收机最多等待多久，约 0.7 s。即使最低画质，这么大的图像也需要约每 16x16 块 160 bit/s 才能及时到达；低于此值编码器无法生成有效码流。要更低码率，请选更小的图像，或在此 FFmpeg 支持时选 H.264 / H.265。
MPEG-TS file (.ts)	MPEG-TS 文件（.ts）
MPEG-TS file (.ts): a transport stream file; give it to the transmitter as its "MPEG-TS / video file", it is sent as it is. UDP or RTP stream: sent over the network in real time, to watch in VLC or feed another program. RTP adds a small header with a sequence number, so a player can see lost packets.	MPEG-TS 文件（.ts）：传输流文件；交给发射机作为其「MPEG-TS / 视频文件」，按原样发送。UDP 或 RTP 流：通过网络实时发送，可在 VLC 中观看或供其他程序使用。RTP 附加带序号的小头部，播放器可看出丢包。

Mark your own meters and tyre sensors. Only marked sensors are followed: passing cars and the neighbours' meters are never tracked. The figures start when the app starts.	标记你自己的表计和胎压传感器。只跟踪已标记的传感器：过路车辆和邻居的表计永远不会被跟踪。数据从应用启动时开始统计。

Maximal-ratio (adds both)	最大比合并（两路相加）
Maximal-ratio adds the two signals in phase so the noise partly cancels: up to 3 dB better with two equal radios. Selection simply uses the better radio and is the safe choice when the two do not hear the same signal.	最大比合并将两路信号同相相加，噪声部分抵消：两台相同电台最多好 3 dB。选择式合并直接用较好的那台，在两台收不到同一信号时是稳妥选择。

Meteosat high rate data collection platforms: a 2 s carrier, then the message at 1200 bit/s offset QPSK with a convolutional and a Reed-Solomon code and a CRC, on a 3 kHz assignment of 402.2 to 402.435 MHz. The 8-digit hex address names the platform.	Meteosat 高速率数据收集平台：2 s 载波，然后是 1200 bit/s 偏移 QPSK 消息，带卷积码、Reed-Solomon 码和 CRC，占用 402.2 至 402.435 MHz 中 3 kHz 指配。8 位十六进制地址标识平台。

Mismatch dB	失配 dB
Mismatch: what is left of the two radios' difference in their overlap after the correction (-30 dB good, above -10 dB poor). It needs a signal both radios hear in the overlap: with only noise there it stays poor, and that is fine.	失配：校正后两台电台重叠区差异的残余（-30 dB 良好，高于 -10 dB 差）。需要两台电台在重叠区都能收到的信号：只有噪声时会一直偏差，这没关系。

Mono saves some bitrate; stereo needs more.	单声道可节省一些码率；立体声则需要更多码率。
More bitrate generally gives a better picture but uses more of the channel. Auto follows a rule of thumb for this codec, resolution, frame rate and frame types; the estimate on the right shows what the whole stream then needs. Untick Auto to set your own. MPEG-2 has a lowest bitrate for each picture size (about 160 bit/s per 16x16 block, e.g. 150 kbit/s at 640x360): a picture must reach the receiver within about 0.7 s.	更高码率通常画质更好，但占用更多信道。自动按此编码、分辨率、帧率和帧类型的经验规则取值；右侧估算值显示整条码流的需求。取消勾选「自动」可自行设置。MPEG-2 对每种图像大小有最低码率（约每 16x16 块 160 bit/s，640x360 时约 150 kbit/s）：图像必须在约 0.7 s 内到达接收机。

When on, the landing prediction may fetch:\n- elevation tiles (1 x 1 degree SRTM, s3.amazonaws.com/elevation-tiles-prod, 10 to 25 MB each, kept in the terrain folder),\n- pressure-level winds of the GFS model for up to three points near the flight (api.open-meteo.com; it receives those points, rounded to 0.25 degrees, and the hour), at most every 2 minutes, kept in the sonde-wind folder.\nNothing else is sent. Offline mode (Settings) turns all of it off.	默认关闭。开启后，落地预测可能会获取：\n- 高程瓦片（1 x 1 度 SRTM，s3.amazonaws.com/elevation-tiles-prod，每块 10 至 25 MB，保存在 terrain 文件夹），\n- GFS 模式飞行附近最多三个点的气压层风（api.open-meteo.com；它会收到这些点，取整到 0.25 度，以及小时），最多每 2 分钟一次，保存在 sonde-wind 文件夹。\n不发送任何其他内容。离线模式（设置）会关闭全部。

On a HackRF the firmware does the hopping (hackrf_sweep style), which is several times faster than retuning from the computer. Receive only.	在 HackRF 上跳频由固件完成（hackrf_sweep 方式），比从计算机重新调谐快几倍。仅接收。
On a HackRF the firmware retunes by itself for spans over 30 MHz (much faster, receive only, no hit recording). Falls back to the normal retune loop when the radio cannot do it.	在 HackRF 上，跨度超过 30 MHz 时由固件自行重新调谐（快得多，仅接收，无命中记录）。电台不支持时回退到普通重调谐循环。
On a Windows PC, install Icom's free "USB I/Q Package for HDSDR"; it puts IC-R8600_usb_iq.spt in C:\\Windows\\System32\\IC-R8600\\.	在 Windows PC 上安装 Icom 免费的「USB I/Q Package for HDSDR」；它会把 IC-R8600_usb_iq.spt 放到 C:\\Windows\\System32\\IC-R8600\\。
On a weak station the stereo part carries most of the hiss: Auto mixes it down towards mono as the signal gets weaker. Pick Stereo below to keep full stereo.	弱信号时立体声部分带有大部分嘶声：信号变弱时「自动」会将其向单声道混合。在下方选「立体声」可保持完整立体声。

On the radio's computer run SoapySDRServer --bind="0.0.0.0:55132" (port 55132, open it in the firewall). The samples go over UDP by default, which works on a LAN; through the internet, a VPN or NAT add prot=tcp to the field (driver=sdrplay,prot=tcp). Client and server should run the same SoapyRemote version.	在电台所在计算机上运行 SoapySDRServer --bind="0.0.0.0:55132"（端口 55132，在防火墙中放行）。采样默认经 UDP 传输，适用于局域网；经互联网时，VPN 或 NAT 需在字段中加 prot=tcp（driver=sdrplay,prot=tcp）。客户端和服务端应运行相同版本的 SoapyRemote。

On: the other signals in the capture stay off. Off: everything found is decoded as well.	开：捕获中的其他信号保持关闭。关：发现的全部内容也会被解码。
OnAir comes with no channels. You are responsible for what you open; use sources you may watch.	OnAir 不附带频道。你须对打开的内容负责；请使用有权观看的信号源。
OnAir connects only to the broker you type here, and only while this is ticked. There is no default server.	OnAir 只连接你在此输入的代理，且仅在此勾选时连接。没有默认服务器。
OnAir decrypts a call only with a key you add here. A call that names a key id uses only keys with that id; a call that does not is tried against your enabled keys for its protocol, in this order. OnAir never searches for a key.	OnAir 只用你在此添加的密钥解密呼叫。呼叫指明密钥 ID 时只使用该 ID 的密钥；未指明时按其协议依次尝试你启用的密钥。OnAir 从不搜索密钥。

OnAir did not read the input ring fast enough, so samples the radio had already delivered were thrown away.	OnAir 读取输入环形缓冲不够快，电台已交付的采样被丢弃。
OnAir does not hold your LoTW certificate. With TQSL installed, OnAir writes the QSOs not yet sent to a file and asks TQSL to sign and upload it; TQSL uses your own certificate and station location.	OnAir 不保存你的 LoTW 证书。安装 TQSL 后，OnAir 把尚未发送的 QSO 写入文件并请求 TQSL 签名上传；TQSL 使用你自己的证书和电台位置。
OnAir drives the SPECTRAN V6 and V6 ECO through Aaronia's RTSA API library, which comes with the RTSA-Suite PRO. OnAir cannot include it.	OnAir 通过 Aaronia 的 RTSA API 库驱动 SPECTRAN V6 和 V6 ECO，该库随 RTSA-Suite PRO 提供。OnAir 无法将其打包。
OnAir keeps a log of what each mode hears (times, ids, quality) in its data folder, only on this computer. Pager ids are stored as short tags, message text is not stored, and audio is not recorded unless you turn it on. You can switch it off per mode or delete it in the Items tab.	OnAir 在其数据文件夹中记录每个模式听到的内容（时间、ID、质量），仅保存在本机。寻呼 ID 存为短标签，不存储消息文本，除非你开启否则不录音。可按模式关闭，或在「项目」选项卡中删除。
OnAir keeps the mode it has.	OnAir 保留当前模式。
OnAir loaded the RX888 firmware, and the radio then reconnects as USB ID 04B4:00F1. The Cypress driver from the official RX-888 setup (the one HDSDR and ExtIO use) has that device. OnAir talks to the radio with libusb instead, and it does not ship a driver.	OnAir 已加载 RX888 固件，随后电台会以 USB ID 04B4:00F1 重新连接。官方 RX-888 安装包（HDSDR 和 ExtIO 使用的那个）中的 Cypress 驱动支持该设备。OnAir 改用 libusb 与电台通信，不附带驱动。
OnAir looks at the spectrum in the background every few seconds, spots signals it knows and labels them. Nothing is decoded until you press Decode.	OnAir 每隔几秒在后台查看频谱，发现认识的信号并加以标注。在你按「解码」前不会解码任何内容。
OnAir never fetches datasets by itself. With this switched on, the buttons below fetch a small manifest (manifest.json) from the sources you list, and only from them. Each file is checked against the sha256 and size in the manifest before it is installed. The data you imported yourself is never replaced without your click.	OnAir 从不自行获取数据集。开启此开关后，下方按钮从你列出的来源获取一个小清单（manifest.json），且仅从那些来源获取。每个文件在安装前都对照清单中的 sha256 和大小校验。你自行导入的数据未经你点击绝不会被替换。
OnAir now uses the new Dev A look. You can switch back in View > Theme.	OnAir 现在使用新的 Dev A 外观。可在「视图 > 主题」中切回。
OnAir plays television that is streamed over the internet. It comes with no channels: add a playlist (M3U / M3U8) from your provider or another source you are allowed to use, or paste the address of one stream. You are responsible for what you open.	OnAir 播放通过互联网串流的电视。它不附带频道：添加你提供商或其他有权使用的来源的播放列表（M3U / M3U8），或粘贴某个串流的地址。你须对打开的内容负责。
OnAir relay only (a stable link, no open ports)	仅 OnAir 中继（链路稳定，无开放端口）
OnAir ships no repeater list. Download one from Open Repeater with your own free API key, or export a CSV from a repeater directory you use (columns for the output frequency and the position) and import it here. It stays on this computer.	OnAir 不附带中继台列表。用你自己的免费 API 密钥从 Open Repeater 下载，或从你使用的中继目录导出 CSV（含输出频率和位置的列）并导入此处。数据只保留在本机。
OnAir talks to the IC-R8600 I/Q port over USB with libusb. On Windows it needs the WinUSB driver for both USB IDs: 0C26:0022 (before the firmware) and 0C26:0023 (running). Icom's own driver, HDSDR and OnAir cannot share the port.	OnAir 通过 libusb 经 USB 与 IC-R8600 的 I/Q 端口通信。Windows 上两个 USB ID 都需要 WinUSB 驱动：0C26:0022（固件加载前）和 0C26:0023（运行中）。Icom 自己的驱动、HDSDR 和 OnAir 不能共享该端口。
OnAir talks to the RX888 MkII over USB with libusb. On Windows the radio needs the WinUSB driver from Zadig for both USB IDs: 04B4:00F3 (boot loader) and 04B4:00F1 (running, "RX888mk2"). Use a USB 3 port; official packages include SDDC_FX3.img in the codecs folder.	OnAir 通过 libusb 经 USB 与 RX888 MkII 通信。Windows 上该电台需要 Zadig 的 WinUSB 驱动，两个 USB ID 都需要：04B4:00F3（引导加载程序）和 04B4:00F1（运行中，「RX888mk2」）。请使用 USB 3 口；官方安装包在 codecs 文件夹附带 SDDC_FX3.img。
OnAir transmits only after you press Arm. It warns you when no licence is set or the signal lies outside an amateur band or your country's plan, and then leaves the decision to you: you are responsible for being allowed to transmit. Your own licence class and band plan are yours to follow.	OnAir 只在你按「预备」后才发射。未设置执照或信号位于业余频段或你国家的规划之外时它会警告你，然后把决定权留给你：你须自行确保有权发射。你自己的执照等级和频段规划由你遵守。

One click: Auto gain, this mode's own bandwidth and sample rate, and the frequency nudged onto the signal when it is a narrow one. Every step can be undone.	一键：自动增益、此模式自身的带宽和采样率，窄信号时频率自动对齐。每一步都可撤销。

One rule: while you transmit, and for 10 minutes after, nothing is uploaded to the OnAir map server, and nothing you transmitted in the last 24 hours (callsign, MMSI, ICAO address) is uploaded. Decoding on your screen goes on.	一条规则：发射期间及之后 10 分钟内，不向 OnAir 地图服务器上传任何内容，过去 24 小时你发射的内容（呼号、MMSI、ICAO 地址）也不上传。屏幕上的解码照常进行。

Peak ridge	峰值脊线
Peak the dish on a DVB-S signal from the satellite (signal meter or OnAir lock). Leave the rotator as it is — even if it reads 0° when you are not on north. OnAir compares the true sky direction to the mechanical reading and stores the offset.	用卫星的 DVB-S 信号对星（信号表或 OnAir 锁定）。保持转台原状——即使你没朝北而它读 0° 也不要动。OnAir 比较真实天空方向与机械读数并存储偏移。
Peer to peer: another station connects to you on the selected entry's link and frequency (tested between two OnAir stations only).	点对点：另一台站通过所选条目的链路和频率连接你（仅在两台 OnAir 台站之间测试过）。
Per Hz (the noise floor stays put when the FFT size changes)	按 Hz（FFT 大小改变时噪声底保持不变）
Per band	按频段
Per continent	按大洲
Per hour of the day	按一天中的小时
Per mode	按模式
Per system, set the parameters you want or leave them on Any. The list beside them shows the modes that carry the bitrate within the bandwidth at the C/N, the best first: the smallest bandwidth, then the most C/N margin. Satellite modes have a free symbol rate: their bandwidth is what the bitrate needs, their capacity what the whole bandwidth would carry. The thresholds are the ideal AWGN values of the standards (no implementation margin, phase noise or multipath): keep a few dB in hand. The DVB-T2 bitrate is the ideal cell capacity (P1/P2 symbols and signalling not deducted), so the real one is a few percent lower.	按系统设置你要的参数，或保持「任意」。旁边的列表显示在此 C/N 下能在该带宽内承载此码率的模式，最优在前：先带宽最小，再 C/N 余量最大。卫星模式的符号率自由：其带宽是码率所需，容量是整个带宽能承载的量。阈值为各标准的理想 AWGN 值（无实现余量、相位噪声或多径）：请预留几 dB。DVB-T2 码率为理想单元容量（未扣除 P1/P2 符号和信令），实际值低几个百分点。
Per track. 64 to 96 kbit/s is fine for speech in mono, 128 to 192 kbit/s for music in stereo (MPEG-1 Layer II needs the higher end).	按音轨。单声道语音 64 至 96 kbit/s 即可，立体声音乐 128 至 192 kbit/s（MPEG-1 Layer II 需要接近上限）。

Periods are from memory and drift with the Earth's motion; the search covers 0.02 % around the period. Check the ATNF pulsar catalogue before a long run.	周期值来自已有记录，并会随地球运动而漂移；搜索范围覆盖该周期上下 0.02 %。长时间运行前请核对 ATNF 脉冲星目录。

Pick your own home ID: its nodes get retransmissions, missing acknowledgements, signal trend and silence. Other networks are not followed.	选择你自己的家庭 ID：其节点可获得重传、缺失确认、信号趋势和静默信息。不跟踪其他网络。
Pick your own network (PAN): its nodes get retransmissions, missing acknowledgements, signal trend and silence. Other networks are not followed. Encrypted networks work too: only the MAC header is used.	选择你自己的网络（PAN）：其节点可获得重传、缺失确认、信号趋势和静默信息。不跟踪其他网络。加密网络也可：仅使用 MAC 头部。

Pictures per second. Lower rates need less data for motion but look less smooth. Some equipment takes only certain rates: 25 fps in 50 Hz countries, 30 fps in 60 Hz countries.	每秒图像数。较低的帧率运动所需数据更少但不够平滑。有些设备只接受特定帧率：50 Hz 国家 25 fps，60 Hz 国家 30 fps。

Place signals at fixed frequencies, each with its own mode: they are decoded together from this capture, and stop by themselves when you tune away.	把信号放在固定频率，各自带自己的模式：它们会从此捕获中一起解码，当你调离时自动停止。

Privacy	隐私
Privacy: SondeHub publishes your uploader callsign and, with the box ticked, your station position on tracker.sondehub.org; the sonde data become part of its open database (CC BY-SA). Only frames with a GPS fix and the sonde's own time are sent; iMet-1/4 are not.	隐私：SondeHub 会公开你的上传者呼号，勾选后还会在 tracker.sondehub.org 公开你的台站位置；探空仪数据将成为其开放数据库的一部分（CC BY-SA）。仅发送带 GPS 定位且为探空仪自身时间的帧；iMet-1/4 不发。
Privacy: a DX cluster and the Reverse Beacon Network are telnet servers that ask for your callsign; OnAir sends only that. POTA and SOTA spots are read from their public pages; nothing about you is sent. PSK Reporter is asked which stations heard your callsign (the callsign is in the request). Nothing runs while it is off.	隐私：DX 集群和反向信标网络是询问你呼号的 telnet 服务器；OnAir 只发送呼号。POTA 和 SOTA 通报从其公开页面读取；不会发送任何关于你的信息。PSK Reporter 只被询问哪些台站听到了你的呼号（呼号在请求中）。关闭时一切都不会运行。
Privacy: aggregators publish what you send, and they know your station's position from your account there. Beast and NMEA data carry no key of yours; enter only the address and key your own account gave you. Nothing is sent until you tick a target.	隐私：聚合商会公开你发送的内容，并从你在那边的账号获知台站位置。Beast 和 NMEA 数据不携带你的密钥；只输入你自己账号给的地址和密钥。勾选目标前不会发送任何内容。
Privacy: the location stays on this computer unless you turn on sending to a public map yourself.	隐私：位置只保留在本机，除非你自行开启发送到公共地图。
Privacy: what is said on public aviation radio is public, but the speech engine runs here on your computer and the text is kept in your data folder. Do not use it on private conversations. It is on for what you are listening to (FM, AM, marine, amateur, CB, digital voice ...) once a speech model is installed; untick the boxes below to stop it. A visible line shows what it is doing.	隐私：公共航空电台上说的话是公开的，但语音引擎在你的计算机上本地运行，文本保存在你的数据文件夹中。请勿用于私人谈话。安装语音模型后，它对你收听的内容（FM、AM、海事、业余、CB、数字语音……）生效；取消勾选下方各框可停止。有一行可见状态显示它在做什么。
Privacy: when a service is switched on, each logged QSO is sent to it over HTTPS: the other station's callsign and grid, mode, band and frequency, the reports, the UTC time, and your own callsign and grid. Nothing else, and nothing at all while a service is off. Your keys and passwords stay on this computer and go only to that service. LoTW is not supported (it needs TQSL certificates): export the ADIF log and sign it with TQSL.	隐私：开启某项服务后，每条记录的 QSO 都会通过 HTTPS 发送给它：对方呼号和网格、模式、频段和频率、报告、UTC 时间，以及你自己的呼号和网格。别无其他，服务关闭时完全不发送。你的密钥和密码留在本机，只发给该服务。不支持 LoTW（需要 TQSL 证书）：导出 ADIF 日志并用 TQSL 签名。

Problem	问题
Processing time of a block versus its duration, on the sample thread. Near 100% the receiver is just keeping up. High load and lost samples are separate: load can be high with every sample kept, and samples can be lost while load is low.	采样线程上某模块的处理时间与其时长的比值。接近 100% 时接收机只是刚好跟上。高负载和丢采样是两回事：每个采样都保留时负载也可能很高，负载低时也可能丢采样。

SELCAL (ARINC 714) is a call of two pairs of tones, 1 s each, from 16 tones (letters A B C D E F G H J K L M P Q R S, 312.6 - 1479.1 Hz). A code belongs to one aircraft: it carries no position, so no map. Put a file selcal_aircraft.tsv (CODE, tab, registration) in the data folder to see the aircraft.	SELCAL（ARINC 714）是由两对音调组成的呼叫，每个 1 s，从 16 个音调中选取（字母 A B C D E F G H J K L M P Q R S，312.6 - 1479.1 Hz）。一个代码只属于一架飞机：不含位置，因此没有地图。在数据文件夹放入 selcal_aircraft.tsv（代码、制表符、注册号）即可看到飞机。

Sends WSPR stations you decode to wsprnet.org (HTTP), so they appear on the WSPR map and database. Each spot includes the sender's callsign, frequency, SNR, grid and power; with your callsign and locator. Uploads are queued with a short pause between posts; the same station in the same two-minute slot is sent once. Nothing is sent until you tick the box. Spots are public: use your own valid amateur radio callsign.	把你解码到的 WSPR 台站发送到 wsprnet.org（HTTP），使其出现在 WSPR 地图和数据库中。每条通报含发送方呼号、频率、SNR、网格和功率；并附你的呼号和网格定位。上传排队进行，帖子间有短暂间隔；同一台站在同一两分钟时隙只发一次。勾选前不发送任何内容。接收报告是公开的：请使用你自己的有效业余电台呼号。
Sends the 6-character locator square of your receiver position (about 5 x 2.5 km) with each post, so the map can measure how far you hear. The map uses it for that post and does not store it. Off: no range badges.	随每次上报发送接收机位置的 6 字符网格（约 5 x 2.5 km），地图用它衡量你能听多远。地图仅将其用于该次上报，不做存储。关：无距离徽章。

Setup	设置
Setup and lessons	设置与课程
Several OnAir stations with radios time the same lightning crackle; one of them, the server, finds the strike from the differences in arrival time. It needs timing to a microsecond at each station: a GPS-disciplined radio, or a VLF / LF time reference all stations can receive. Tune the band with the reference in it (around 20 to 60 kHz) and pick a role under the decoder options. Receive only.	多个带电台的 OnAir 台站对同一声雷闪计时；其中作为服务器的台站根据到达时间差定位雷击。每个台站都需要微秒级计时：GPS 驯服电台，或所有台站都能收到的 VLF / LF 授时信号。调到含该参考信号的频段（约 20 至 60 kHz）并在译码器选项下选择角色。仅接收。
Several demodulators run side by side. When none of them gets a frame right, their copies are voted bit by bit and the few bits they disagree on are tried until the check fits. More frames at the edge of reception; frames made this way are marked [repaired by vote]. On by default.	多个解调器并排运行。没有一个能正确解出一帧时，对它们的副本逐位投票，并对有分歧的少数位逐个尝试直到校验通过。接收边缘能解出更多帧；这样修复的帧标有 [repaired by vote]。默认开启。
Several modes at once	多个模式同时
Several narrow radios (for example 5 RTL-SDRs) tuned side by side with some overlap become one wide radio in the list. Fine for the spectrum, waterfall, FM/AM, narrow voice and scanning; wide digital TV across the seams is experimental. See docs/SYNTH-WIDEBAND.md.	多台窄带电台（例如 5 个 RTL-SDR）并排调谐并留少量重叠，在列表中合成一台宽带电台。频谱、瀑布图、FM/AM、窄带语音和扫描都很好用；跨接缝的宽带数字电视是实验性的。见 docs/SYNTH-WIDEBAND.md。

Slot modes with a CAT rig: for each transmission the rig's dial moves in 500 Hz steps so your audio stays between 1500 and 2000 Hz (cleaner, no harmonics in the passband), and comes back after. The RF frequency is the same.	带 CAT 电台的 slot 模式：每次发射时电台刻度以 500 Hz 步进移动，使你的音频保持在 1500 至 2000 Hz 之间（更干净，通带内无谐波），发射后恢复。射频频率不变。

SoapySDR radios (LimeSDR, bladeRF, USRP, XTRX, ...): set a transmit gain stage by its driver name after the overall gain, for example PAD=40, IAMP=0. SoapySDR 电台（LimeSDR、bladeRF、USRP、XTRX……）：在总增益之后按驱动名设置发射增益级，例如 PAD=40、IAMP=0。每一项只被钳制到驱动报告的范围；驱动未列出的名称仍会发送，日志会有说明。留空：只用总增益。

TCI: the Transceiver Control Interface of ExpertSDR3 (and SunSDR or other programs with a TCI server), port 50001. Turn its TCI server on and type that computer's address. TCI：ExpertSDR3（以及 SunSDR 或其他带 TCI 服务器的程序）的收发机控制接口，端口 50001。打开其 TCI 服务器并输入该计算机的地址。OnAir 只接收（IQ 流，48 至 384 kHz）；从不发射。无需密码。

The 20-minute cycle 160, 80, 60, 40, 30, 20, 17, 15, 12, 10 m, one band per 2-minute slot from the hour, as WSJT-X's coordinated hopping. A band you switched off gives its slot to the next one. Your own frequencies are not part of that cycle: with this on they are used only in an hour where no standard band is ticked; switch it off to hop to them in turn.	20 分钟循环：160、80、60、40、30、20、17、15、12、10 m，从整点起每 2 分钟一个频段，与 WSJT-X 的协调跳频一致。你关掉的频段会把时间让给下一个。你自己的频率不属于该循环：开启时它们只在没有勾选标准频段的整点使用；关闭则轮流跳到它们。
The ATSC pilot sits 309.44 kHz above the lower channel edge; the radio is tuned to it. A main carrier, accurate to a fraction of a ppm.	ATSC 导频位于信道下边缘上方 309.44 kHz；电台已调谐到它。一个精确到零点几 ppm 的主载波。

The Dropbox link with the ready-built codec files is posted in the Discord: download the files for your system, put them in the codecs folder and press Look again.	预编译编解码器文件的 Dropbox 链接发布在 Discord 中：下载适合你系统的文件，放入 codecs 文件夹，再按一次「查找」。
The Flipper's Sub-GHz chat: CC1101 GFSK, 9.99 kbaud, 19 kHz deviation, sync word 0x464C, packets of up to 60 octets with a CRC-16. Packets less than 0.5 s apart make one message, as the Flipper app joins them. Encrypted chats use AES-256-GCM with a key from a password (SHA-256) or typed in hex, and drop repeated messages.	Flipper 的 Sub-GHz 聊天：CC1101 GFSK，9.99 kbaud，频偏 19 kHz，同步字 0x464C，最长 60 八位组带 CRC-16 的数据包。间隔小于 0.5 秒的数据包合成一条消息（与 Flipper 应用一致）。加密聊天使用 AES-256-GCM，密钥来自密码（SHA-256）或手输十六进制，并丢弃重复消息。
The General receiver listens to any frequency. Its filter width (bandwidth) decides how much around the frequency you hear: narrow cuts noise and neighbours, wide lets a broad signal through.\nPick another width in the mode panel.	通用接收机收听任意频率。其滤波器宽度（带宽）决定频率周围能听到多少：窄可滤掉噪声和邻台，宽可通过宽带信号。\n在模式面板中选择其他宽度。
The HFDL Nearby view and the automatic band choice ask hfdl.observer which frequencies the ground stations use now: one HTTPS request to hfdl.observer/active.json at most every few minutes, only while the view is open or a band choice is automatic, with the header User-Agent: OnAir/<version>. The site sees your IP address and the app version; your position is never sent. Off: the squitters this receiver hears only. Never in Offline mode.	HFDL Nearby 视图和自动频段选择会向 hfdl.observer 询问地面站当前使用的频率：最多每隔几分钟向 hfdl.observer/active.json 发一次 HTTPS 请求，仅在此视图打开或自动选频段时，请求头 User-Agent: OnAir/<版本>。该网站会看到你的 IP 和应用版本；你的位置绝不发送。关：只用此接收机听到的广播。离线模式下永不请求。
The HRDCP assignments lie in 402.2005 - 402.4345 MHz: 0.25 Msps around 402.3 MHz holds them all.	HRDCP 指配位于 402.2005 - 402.4345 MHz：402.3 MHz 附近 0.25 Msps 即可覆盖全部。
The Hamlib model number (rigctl -l), typed directly. IC-R8600 is 3079, IC-7300 is 3073, 2 is a rigctld on the network.	Hamlib 模型编号（rigctl -l），直接输入。IC-R8600 是 3079，IC-7300 是 3073，2 是网络上的 rigctld。
The I/Q OUT USB port of the IC-R8600 has no program of its own: it needs Icom's firmware each time it is plugged in. OnAir cannot include it (it is Icom's), so you bring the file once.	IC-R8600 的 I/Q OUT USB 口没有自己的程序：每次插入都需要 Icom 的固件。OnAir 无法附带它（它是 Icom 的），所以你需要自己提供一次该文件。
The I/Q OUT port of the IC-R8600 has no program of its own: OnAir loads Icom's firmware into its RAM each time (nothing is flashed to the radio). The file is Icom's, so OnAir cannot include it.	IC-R8600 的 I/Q OUT 口没有自己的程序：OnAir 每次把 Icom 的固件加载进它的 RAM（不会刷写到电台）。文件是 Icom 的，OnAir 无法附带。

The Mode Builder makes a decoder from a description of a digital protocol: modulation, sync word, frame length, error correction, check value and fields. Build one, test it on a recording or on a test signal made from it, then register it: it is listed here as a mode of its own.	模式构建器根据数字协议的描述生成译码器：调制、同步字、帧长、纠错、校验值和字段。构建一个，在录音或用它生成的测试信号上测试，然后注册：它会作为独立模式列在这里。

The PC that receives the stream (unicast, e.g. 192.168.1.20) or a multicast group (239.0.0.0 to 239.255.255.255) that any PC on the network can join. The beacon's modulation as this receiver measures it, averaged over 20 s, against the ICAO Annex 10 limits. A receiver is not a flight check: filters, AGC and reflections move the figures a little, so a value just outside a limit is shown as marginal. The limits are quoted from memory of Annex 10 Vol I.	此接收机测得、20 秒平均的信标调制，对照 ICAO 附件 10 限值。接收机不是飞行校验：滤波器、AGC 和反射会让数值略有偏移，因此略超限值显示为边缘。限值凭对附件 10 第一卷的记忆引用。

The mode with the smallest bandwidth that carries the bitrate at this C/N, over all systems and with the parameters set in their blocks; between modes of the same bandwidth, the one with the most C/N margin. The network radio is not delivering its full rate. Use a wired connection, or a lower sample rate.	网络电台未提供完整速率。请使用有线连接或更低采样率。
The network radio's stream was down (a reconnect): those samples never reached OnAir. This is the link, not the CPU.	网络电台的流中断了（重连中）：那些采样从未到达 OnAir。这是链路问题，不是 CPU。
The newest clips are kept; older ones are deleted automatically. A starred clip is always kept.	保留最新片段；较旧的自动删除。加星的片段永远保留。
The newest phase steps of the first carriers (one digit per symbol, 0 to 3 = steps of 90 degrees in D-QPSK):	前几个载波最新的相位步进（每个符号一个数字，0 至 3 = D-QPSK 中 90 度步进）：
The noise floor is %.1f dB above the ADC's own noise (%.1f dBFS per bin). Below about %.0f dB more gain buys sensitivity; far above it, gain only costs headroom.	噪声底比 ADC 自身噪声高 %.1f dB（每 bin %.1f dBFS）。低于约 %.0f dB 时加增益可换灵敏度；远高于它时增益只损失余量。
The noise floor is the grainy hiss everywhere in the spectrum. A signal is anything that stands out above it.	噪声底就是频谱上颗粒状的嘶声。信号是任何突出于其上的东西。
The noise floor stays put when the FFT size changes. Off: dB per bin.	FFT 大小改变时噪声底保持不变。关：按每 bin 计 dB。
The offset includes your receiver's own clock error. A GPS-locked or calibrated radio gives the tightest match; with an uncalibrated radio keep the allowance wide or rely on the key-up transient, pilot and spurs.	偏移包含你接收机自身的时钟误差。GPS 锁定或校准过的电台匹配最紧；未校准时保持容限放宽，或依靠按键瞬态、导频和杂散。
The packet ID of the first audio track; further tracks follow it.	第一条音轨的包 ID；后续音轨紧随其后。
The packet ID of the video stream.	视频流的包 ID。
The packet IDs of the PMT, the video and the audio. Automatic gives one programme with the usual PIDs (PMT 0x1000, video 0x100, audio 0x101). Set them yourself to test a receiver's handling of specific PIDs.	PMT、视频和音频的包 ID。自动给出一个节目，使用常用 PID（PMT 0x1000，视频 0x100，音频 0x101）。自行设置可测试接收机对特定 PID 的处理。
The page the teletext view opens on.	图文电视视图打开时显示的页。
The part of OnAir that turns a received signal back into sound, pictures, text or positions. Each mode has its own.	OnAir 中把接收到的信号还原成声音、图像、文本或位置的部分。每个模式都有自己的译码器。
The passband touches the edge of the recorded band.	通带触及录音频段的边缘。
The passphrase needs at least 8 characters.	口令至少需要 8 个字符。
The password and tokens are stored in the integrations folder of your data folder (readable by you only). They are never put into a log, a message or this window. Nothing is sent to the OnAir map by this window.	密码和令牌保存在数据文件夹的 integrations 文件夹中（仅你可读）。它们绝不会写入日志、消息或此窗口。此窗口不会向 OnAir 地图发送任何内容。
The password is too short: 8 characters at least.	密码太短：至少 8 个字符。
The payload is not valid hex.	有效载荷不是有效的十六进制。
The peaks are at full scale: the limiter rounds them in a real transmission.	峰值已到满幅：真实发射中限幅器会将其削圆。
The phase steps of the newest burst on the first carriers (one digit per symbol; 0 / 1 in D-BPSK, 0 to 3 = steps of 90 degrees in D-QPSK):	前几个载波最新突发的相位步进（每个符号一个数字；D-BPSK 为 0 / 1，D-QPSK 中 0 至 3 = 90 度步进）：
The physical layer scrambling sequence number n of the TDM waveform; 0 is the default of a single-beam system.	TDM 波形的物理层扰码序列号 n；单波束系统默认为 0。
The pitch of the CW tone you hear. The receiver makes 700 Hz; this moves it.	你听到的 CW 音调音高。接收机定为 700 Hz，此项移动它。
The pop-out video window stays above other windows.	弹出视频窗口始终位于其他窗口之上。
The port you chose is already in use.	你选择的端口已被占用。
The port's position gives the distance and an ETA from each vessel's speed. Without it only the declared ETA is shown.	港口位置给出距离，并按每艘船的速度估算 ETA。没有它则只显示船方申报的 ETA。
The preview failed.	预览失败。
The priority channel is checked every few seconds, also while another channel holds	优先信道每隔几秒检查一次，即使另一个信道正在保持
The programme number in the PAT and PMT, and the service ID in the SDT. Receivers use it to find the programme; 1 is fine unless you test something specific.	PAT 和 PMT 中的节目号，及 SDT 中的业务 ID。接收机用它找到节目；设为 1 即可，除非要测试特定内容。
The provider name in the SDT; some receivers show it with the channel.	SDT 中的提供商名称；有些接收机会随频道显示它。

The quality level: lower is better and costs more bits. 18 is close to transparent, 23 is the x264 default, 28 is clearly softer.	质量级别：越低越好、码率越高。18 接近透明，23 是 x264 默认值，28 明显偏柔。
The radio did not start the last time. Help > Troubleshooting and the How to fix box next to the error explain the usual causes (driver, permission, USB cable).	电台上次未能启动。帮助 > 故障排除和错误旁「如何修复」框解释了常见原因（驱动、权限、USB 线）。

The radio is losing samples. Use a USB 3 port, a short cable, or a lower sample rate.	电台正在丢样。请使用 USB 3 口、短线，或更低采样率。

The radio is running at %s; %s was asked for. The driver rounded the rate, or OnAir runs the radio faster on purpose (offset tuning away from the DC spike, Decimate in OnAir) and brings it back to the asked rate itself.	电台正以 %s 运行；请求的是 %s。可能是驱动把速率取整了，也可能是 OnAir 故意让电台跑得更快（偏置调谐避开 DC 尖峰、OnAir 内抽取），然后自己换算回请求速率。
The radio source this block uses	此模块使用的电台信号源
The radio you chose is not connected. Plug it in and press Refresh, or choose another one.	你选择的电台未连接。插入并按「刷新」，或选择另一台。
The radio's analogue filter in front of its transmit converter. Automatic follows the sample rate (about 95 percent of it; the HackRF picks its own nearest width). A width is sent to the driver as typed; the driver takes its nearest.	电台发射变频器前的模拟滤波器。自动跟随采样率（约为其 95%；HackRF 自选最接近的宽度）。宽度按输入原样发给驱动，驱动取其最接近值。
The radio's overall gain	电台总增益
The radio's own TX gain. A Flipper in the same room needs very little; the HackRF's +14 dB amplifier stays off	电台自身的 TX 增益。同一房间的 Flipper 只需很小功率；HackRF 的 +14 dB 放大器保持关闭
The radio's own TX gain. EU 869.4 - 869.65 MHz allows 500 mW ERP, most other 868 MHz sub-bands 25 mW; a HackRF gives a few mW at most (its +14 dB amplifier stays off). Use the least power that works.	电台自身的 TX 增益。欧盟 869.4 - 869.65 MHz 允许 500 mW ERP，868 MHz 其他子频段多为 25 mW；HackRF 最多输出几 mW（其 +14 dB 放大器保持关闭）。使用能通联的最小功率。
The radio's own VOX	电台自身 VOX
The radio's sample rate for this mode, remembered for this radio. The standard rate is the mode's own; tick Custom to choose any rate: the ones the radio lists, or a typed one that goes to the driver as it is. A rate the radio does not list, or below what the decoder needs, only gets a warning.	此模式使用该电台的采样率，按电台记忆。标准速率为模式自身；勾选「自定义」可选任意速率：电台列出的，或输入后原样传给驱动的速率。电台未列出或低于译码器所需时仅警告。
The radio's sound output could not be opened.	电台的声音输出无法打开。
The radiosonde stations of NOAA's IGRA 2 list that launched in 2025 or later, and your own in sonde-sites-known.txt in the data folder (one per line: name; latitude; longitude).	NOAA IGRA 2 列表中 2025 年及以后仍在施放的探空仪站点，以及你自己写在数据文件夹 sonde-sites-known.txt 中的站点（每行：名称；纬度；经度）。
The receive and transmit buffering of the radio, from the moment OnAir hands over the samples to the air. Adjust until the repeater takes the call.	电台的接收和发送缓冲，从 OnAir 交付出采样到空中算起。调整到中继能接受呼叫为止。
The receiver cannot keep up at this sample rate: it needs more than one CPU core gives. Lower the sample rate or the bandwidth.	接收机在此采样率下跟不上：需要一个 CPU 核心以上的算力。降低采样率或带宽。
The receiver is off while a burst goes out and for about a second after it, while it starts again. An ACK that arrives then is missed: a message can show 'no ACK' although it arrived. A radio that receives while it sends (PlutoSDR, USRP, bladeRF, LimeSDR) avoids this.	突发发出期间及其后约一秒重启期间接收机关闭。此时到达的 ACK 会被错过：消息可能显示「未确认」但其实已到。能收发同时进行的电台（PlutoSDR、USRP、bladeRF、LimeSDR）可避免此问题。
The receiver is on the uplink. Tune to 465.9875 MHz (or pick Downlink above) during a MetOp pass; the pass planner (Tools > Satellite passes) lists MetOp-B / -C with the Argos downlink.	接收机在上行链路。MetOp 过境期间调到 465.9875 MHz（或在上方选「下行链路」）；过境规划器（工具 > 卫星过境）列出了带 Argos 下行链路的 MetOp-B / -C。
The receiver is running; the word follows the mode's lock	接收机运行中；字样跟随模式的锁定状态

The receiver's correction of this same radio (its crystal is shared by both directions). Only offered when the receiver uses this radio and has a correction.	同一电台的接收机校正值（收发共用晶振）。仅当接收机使用此电台且有校正值时提供。
The receiving PC (unicast) or a multicast group such as 239.1.1.1	接收方计算机（单播）或组播组，例如 239.1.1.1
The reference data OnAir uses. Your own file layered over a dataset wins over the bundled one (by key for lists), and Reset to bundled removes it again. Map tiles, satellite elements and station lists that you download are in View > Data packs.	OnAir 使用的参考数据。你自己叠放在数据集之上的文件优先于内置文件（列表按键匹配），「恢复内置」可再次移除它。你下载的地图瓦片、卫星根数和台站列表在「视图 > 数据包」中。
The round button at the top left starts and stops the receiver. Nothing is received while it is stopped.	左上角的圆形按钮启动和停止接收机。停止时收不到任何内容。
The same key on every station: packets are signed with it and anything else is dropped. Nothing is sent anywhere but to the server you name.	每个台站使用同一密钥：数据包用它签名，其他一律丢弃。除你指定的服务器外不发送任何内容。
The same position as in every other mode (ADS-B antenna position, Passes, Map). It is marked on the map below.	与其他所有模式相同的位置（ADS-B 天线位置、过境、地图）。它已标在下方地图上。
The sample rate actually handed over by the radio, over the last seconds. A warning appears below 97% of the set rate.	电台最近几秒实际交付的采样率。低于设定值 97% of 时显示警告。
The sample rate sets how wide the spectrum is. A wider view shows more signals at once.	采样率决定频谱有多宽。越宽一次显示的信号越多。
The satellite or link. Picking one tunes to its frequency and sets its modulation and frame format.	卫星或链路。选择一个会调谐到其频率并设置调制和帧格式。
The satellite retransmits the uplink carriers around 1679.85 MHz: one dish hears a hemisphere of platforms. Tested on simulated signals only.	卫星在 1679.85 MHz 附近转发上行载波：一口天线锅可听到半球内的所有平台。仅在模拟信号上测试。

The size of the encoded picture. Lower resolution needs less bitrate and less encoder time. Use a size your receiver supports: 720x576 is standard definition in Europe, 1280x720 and 1920x1080 are HD.	编码图像的大小。更低分辨率需要更少码率和编码时间。使用接收机支持的尺寸：720x576 是欧洲标清，1280x720 和 1920x1080 是高清。

The software that lets the computer talk to the radio. On Windows it is usually WinUSB, installed with Zadig; on Linux, udev rules give your account access.	让计算机与电台通信的软件。Windows 上通常是 Zadig 安装的 WinUSB；Linux 上用 udev 规则授予账号访问权限。

The spectrum is a live graph of signal strength across frequency. The waterfall stacks those graphs over time, so steady and short signals look different.	频谱是信号强度随频率变化的实时图。瀑布图把这些图按时间堆叠，因此稳定信号和短信号看起来不同。

The sweep scanner steps the radio across a wide band and draws everything it finds in one picture. Strong peaks are worth a closer look.	频谱扫描器让步进电台遍历宽带频段，并把发现的一切画在一张图上。强峰值值得细看。

The table is full.	表格已满。
The tables hold typical gain steps; a real tuner differs by several dB from unit to unit. This steps through every gain once (about 30 s, antenna disconnected or any steady noise), measures the real steps and keeps them for this unit. It also tells you when the gain control does not work.	表中是典型增益步进；真实调谐器逐台相差几 dB。此项逐个遍历所有增益一次（约 30 秒，断开天线或任意稳定噪声），测量真实步进并为本机保留。还会告诉你增益控制是否失效。

The test generator's picture. Test card: colour bars, a moving sweep and a chequerboard with a bouncing ball, so motion and lost packets are easy to see. Colour bars and Grey ramp: still pictures to check colours and levels. A white bar grows along the bottom once a second.	测试发生器的图像。测试卡：彩条、移动扫频和带弹跳球的棋盘格，运动与丢包一目了然。彩条和灰阶：检查色彩和电平的静态图像。底部白条每秒增长一次。

The text is too long for one issue: it goes as %zu issues, one after the other.	文本太长，一个 issue 放不下：将分成 %zu 个 issue 依次提交。
The time between two keyframes (I-frames). A receiver can start or recover only on a keyframe, so a short GOP helps after a dropout and when switching channel; a long GOP compresses better. 1 to 2 s is common for broadcast.	两个关键帧（I 帧）之间的时间。接收机只能在关键帧处开始或恢复，因此短 GOP 有利于中断后和换台；长 GOP 压缩更好。广播常用 1 至 2 秒。
The time, as set in Settings > Time display	时间，按「设置 > 时间显示」中的设置
The times could be off by about %.0f s. Update the orbits.	时间可能偏差约 %.0f 秒。请更新轨道。
The top graph is the spectrum: left is lower frequency, right is higher, up is a stronger signal. Below it the waterfall shows the same over time, newest at the top.\nA signal is a bright bump or stripe that stands out from the grainy noise floor. A steady stripe is a broadcast; short dashes are bursts of data or speech.	上方图是频谱：左低右高，向上信号更强。下方瀑布图显示同样内容随时间的变化，最新在顶部。\n信号是从颗粒状噪声底上突出的亮块或亮条纹。稳定条纹是广播；短划是数据或语音突发。
The top shows the spectrum, and the waterfall scrolls its history below. Click a signal to tune to it.	上方显示频谱，瀑布图在下方滚动历史。点击信号即可调谐过去。
The total TS rate with signalling and null packets. Set it to what the DVB link carries, or let it follow the estimate.	含信令和空包的 TS 总码率。设为 DVB 链路承载量，或跟随估算值。
The total rate of the transport stream: video, audio, tables and null packets that pad it to a constant rate. Keep it at or below the net bitrate of the DVB link, e.g. DVB-T 8 MHz, 64-QAM, code rate 2/3, guard interval 1/4 carries about 19.9 Mbit/s; QPSK 1/2 about 5 Mbit/s. Auto uses the estimate on the right plus a small margin.	传输流总码率：视频、音频、表和填充为恒定速率的空包。保持不超过 DVB 链路净码率，例如 DVB-T 8 MHz、64-QAM、码率 2/3、保护间隔 1/4 约承载 19.9 Mbit/s；QPSK 1/2 约 5 Mbit/s。自动取右侧估算值加少量余量。
The transmitter reads this generator's stream by this name. Several generators can run at once (the tabs at the top); each has its own name: generator:1, generator:2 ...	发射机按此名字读取本发生器的流。可同时运行多个发生器（顶部标签页）；每个有自己的名字：generator:1、generator:2……
The transmitter takes it as its "MPEG-TS / video file".	发射机把它当作「MPEG-TS / 视频文件」。
The transmitter's clock error in parts per million, positive when it runs fast (the signal then goes out above the frequency asked for). Measure it with a known receiver or a frequency counter.	发射机时钟误差，百万分率，偏快时为正（信号实际发出频率高于请求值）。用已知接收机或频率计测量。
The tune box stops at the edge of the recorded band (the passband must stay inside it).	调谐框停在录音频段的边缘（通带必须留在其中）。
The tunnel could not be restarted. Check the internet connection, then stop and start sharing.	隧道无法重启。检查互联网连接，然后停止并重新开始共享。
