The two curves differ by the radio's own frequency error. When the pass planner tracks the pass it retunes for the Doppler, and the measured curve is what is left.	两条曲线的差来自电台自身的频率误差。过境规划器跟踪过境时按多普勒重调谐，实测曲线是剩余部分。
The two passphrases differ.	两个口令不一致。
The unaveraged spectrum, faint, under the averaged line	未平均的频谱，淡淡地衬在平均曲线之下
The up-link is a magnetic field around 4.234 MHz (FSK 3.951 / 4.516 MHz, 564.48 kbit/s). It exists only while a train's 27.095 MHz tele-powering field is over the balise. The radio is tuned 1 MHz higher so the signal stays clear of the DC spike.	上行链路是 4.234 MHz 附近的磁场（FSK 3.951 / 4.516 MHz，564.48 kbit/s）。只在列车 27.095 MHz 遥供电场覆盖到应答器时存在。电台调高 1 MHz 以便信号避开 DC 尖峰。
The uploader callsign needs 3 to 64 characters.	上传者呼号需要 3 至 64 个字符。
The upper sideband is much worse: probably a station on the channel above. The decoder relies on the cleaner side.	上边带差得多：可能是上方信道有电台。译码器依赖较干净的一侧。
The voice codec for this mode is not installed. Calls and data still decode, the sound stays off.	此模式的语音编解码器未安装。呼叫和数据仍可解码，但声音保持关闭。
The volume and mute are at the top right. Move the slider or press the speaker.	音量和静音在右上角。拖动滑块或按扬声器图标。
The waterfall's colours. Navy heat (the default) and Blue green keep the noise floor dark with its texture; the auto range puts the floor where each palette reads best.	瀑布图配色。Navy heat（默认）和 Blue green 让噪声底保持暗色及其纹理；自动范围把噪声底放在每种色图最可读的位置。

The whip hears almost nothing on short wave. FT8 needs a long wire or a loop, and an upconverter for radios that start at 24 MHz.	鞭天线在短波几乎收不到东西。FT8 需要长线或环形天线，起始频率 24 MHz 的电台还需要上变频器。
The width is measured from the spectrum of the channel (7.56 MHz of signal on an 8 MHz channel, 5.67 MHz on a 6 MHz one).	宽度从信道频谱测得（8 MHz 信道上 7.56 MHz 信号，6 MHz 信道上 5.67 MHz）。
The width of the channel around the dial that the analyser looks at; it takes the signal nearest the dial.\nThe standard is 200 kHz. Tick Custom to make it narrower by hand and isolate one signal, or wider.	分析仪查看的刻度周围信道宽度；取离刻度最近的信号。\n标准为 200 kHz。勾选「自定义」可手动收窄以隔离单个信号，或加宽。
The wind the sonde measured on the way up, the fall rate fitted to the descent, a Kalman filter on the drift near the ground,\nthe terrain and (opt-in) a wind forecast, with an uncertainty ellipse. Off: the 3.9 estimate (constant drift, sea-level ground).	探空仪上升途中测得的风、拟合到下降段的下落率、对近地面漂移的卡尔曼滤波，\n地形和（可选）风预报，并给出不确定椭圆。关闭：使用 3.9 估计（恒定漂移、海平面地面）。
The window of the display transforms. Hann: the usual. Blackman-Harris and Nuttall: less leakage beside strong signals, slightly wider bins. Flat top: true levels of tones, wide bins. Rectangular: the narrowest bins, the most leakage.	显示变换的窗函数。Hann：常用。Blackman-Harris 和 Nuttall：强信号旁泄漏更少，bin 略宽。Flat top：音调真实电平，bin 宽。Rectangular：bin 最窄，泄漏最多。
The words you meet on a radio, in plain language. Type to search.	你在电台上遇到的词，用平实语言解释。输入即可搜索。
The world map with the chosen satellites' orbits, footprints and day and night	世界地图，显示所选卫星的轨道、覆盖区和昼夜
The zoom transform asks for about four bins per pixel of the view at most this many. Lower saves CPU on very wide windows.	缩放变换要求视图每像素约 4 个 bin，上限为此值。调低可在很宽的窗口上节省 CPU。

This plugin is unreviewed: anyone with a GitHub account can publish in the community marketplace and nobody has checked this one. It is native code. Once it runs it can do anything this program can do on your computer (read, change or delete your files, use the network), whatever it says it needs; there is no sandbox. Install it only if you trust its author, ideally after reading its source.	此插件未经审核：任何有 GitHub 账号的人都可以在社区市场发布，而这个没人检查过。它是原生代码。一旦运行，它就能做本程序在你计算机上能做的一切（读取、修改或删除你的文件、使用网络），无论它声称需要什么权限；没有沙箱。只在你信任其作者时才安装，最好先读源码。

Tune: press on the spectrum or waterfall to tune there and drag to keep tuning, Shift or Ctrl for fine steps; drag the frequency scale, right- or middle-drag, or the wheel over the scale to pan. Pan: a drag moves the view and a click tunes (the old OnAir).	调谐：在频谱或瀑布图上按下即调过去，拖动持续调谐，Shift 或 Ctrl 细调；拖动频率刻度、右键或中键拖动、或在刻度上滚动以平移。平移模式：拖动移动视图，点击调谐（旧版 OnAir 方式）。
Tuned MHz	已调谐 MHz
Tuned to %.1f MHz %s, the strongest of %d stations.	已调到 %.1f MHz %s，%d 个电台中最强的。
Tuner style	刻度盘样式
Tunes the radio to the middle of the band (or the bottom 8 MHz of a wide one). The VHF bands use 25 kHz channels; the UHF bands use 100 kHz cells. Move the radio with the frequency box to look at another part of a wide band.	把电台调到频段中部（宽带频段则调到底部 8 MHz）。VHF 频段用 25 kHz 信道；UHF 频段用 100 kHz 单元。用频率框移动电台查看宽带频段的其他部分。
Tunes two minutes before each broadcast of the region or station and receives it. Charts are kept by the autosave. A chart under way is never interrupted. Tuning by hand or Stop ends it.	在每个地区或台站广播前两分钟调谐并接收。图像由自动保存保留。进行中的图像永远不会被打断。手动调谐或按「停止」会结束接收。
Turkey	土耳其
Turn Listen on, raise the volume, and choose the output device in the audio output popup.	打开「收听」，调高音量，在音频输出弹窗中选择输出设备。
Turn a mode on to get its packets in Wireshark: as a capture interface ("OnAir: ..." in Wireshark's list) and as a pcapng file. Packets are passed on as received: what is encrypted on the air stays encrypted. Everything stays on this computer.	打开一个模式即可在 Wireshark 中看到它的数据包：作为捕获接口（Wireshark 列表中的「OnAir: …」）和 pcapng 文件。数据包按收到原样传递：空中加密的内容保持加密。一切都留在本机。
Turn it on in the Server menu.	在「服务器」菜单中开启它。
Turn on Track in View > Satellite passes. The mount waits at the rise point before AOS, follows the satellite through the pass (with axis speeds on mounts that take them) and parks or stops at LOS.	在「视图 > 卫星过境」中开启「跟踪」。座架在 AOS 前于升点等待，过境中跟踪卫星（支持轴速度的座架用轴速度），在 LOS 时归位或停止。
Turn on Whole span	开启整个跨度
Turn recording on	开启录制
Turn the picture 90 degrees clockwise (key ]). Applies to the picture on the screen and to the PNGs saved from now on; the setting is remembered.	把图像顺时针转 90 度（] 键）。作用于屏幕上的图像和此后保存的 PNG；设置会被记住。
Turn the picture 90 degrees counter-clockwise (key [).	把图像逆时针转 90 度（[ 键）。
Turn the picture upside down (key \\). Many stations are received with the picture the wrong way up.	把图像上下翻转（\\ 键）。许多台站收到的图像是颠倒的。

Twist: high tone above low (dB)	扭转：高音高于低音（dB）
Two frequencies	两个频率
Two radios tuned to the same frequency (two antennas, or the same antenna through a splitter) are combined into one better signal for narrowband modes such as FM, AM, voice and paging. Off until you make a pair. Needs a signal both radios hear; see docs/DIVERSITY.md.	两台调到同一频率的电台（两根天线，或经功分器共用一根）合并成一个更好的信号，用于 FM、AM、语音和寻呼等窄带模式。组成分集对前关闭。需要两台电台都能收到的信号；见 docs/DIVERSITY.md。

What OnAir does	OnAir 的功能
What Optimise once maximises. The mode's: %s. A decoder number (MER / SNR / BER / lock) is used only while it locks; else the channel SNR from the spectrum.	「优化一次」所最大化的目标。该模式的：%s。解码器数值（MER / SNR / BER / 锁定）仅在其锁定期间使用；否则使用来自频谱的信道 SNR。
What a Sigfox uplink holds	Sigfox 上行链路携带的内容
What antenna this needs	此模式需要的天线
What do you want to listen to?	你想听什么？
What interests you? Tick what you like, or nothing. This sets which modes are listed first, where OnAir starts and a few starter presets.	你对什么感兴趣？勾选喜欢的，或什么都不勾。这会决定哪些模式排在前面、OnAir 从哪里开始以及几个初始预设。
What is encoded. Camera: a webcam or an HDMI / SDI capture card, opened only when you press Start. Still picture: one image sent as a continuous stream (a test card, a slide). Video file: any file FFmpeg can read, played from the start and looped when the length is longer. Test generator: a copy of the transmitter's own moving test picture (the test transport stream the DVB-T2 T2Generator carries in its real PLP). RTMP server: OBS Studio, on this PC or another one on the network, streams its picture and sound to OnAir; nothing else needs to be installed. Put a callsign or other text on it with the Preview tab.	所编码的内容。摄像头：网络摄像头或 HDMI / SDI 采集卡，仅在按「开始」时打开。静态图片：一张图像作为连续码流发送（测试卡、幻灯片）。视频文件：FFmpeg 能读取的任意文件，从头播放，长度播完后循环。测试发生器：发射机自身移动测试画面的副本（DVB-T2 T2Generator 在其实际 PLP 中携带的测试传输流）。RTMP 服务器：本机或网络中另一台 PC 上的 OBS Studio，将其画面和声音推流到 OnAir；无需安装其他东西。用「预览」选项卡在上面叠加呼号或其他文字。
What is sent	发送的内容
What is sent##sondehubjson	发送的内容##sondehubjson
What the audio tracks carry. The test tone (1 kHz at -20 dBFS) is handy to check a receiver's sound. The sound of the video file or of the RTMP stream is only there with a video file or the RTMP server as input. Silence still makes a valid audio track.	音轨承载的内容。测试音（1 kHz，-20 dBFS）便于检查接收机的声音。仅当输入为视频文件或 RTMP 服务器时，才有视频文件或 RTMP 流的声音。静音仍是有效的音轨。
What the link offers. The bitrate is what has to be carried: the generator's TS mux rate unless you untick it. The bandwidth is the room you have on the spectrum (a channel, a band-plan segment). The C/N is the ratio of the carrier to the noise you see on the spectrum analyser, in the same bandwidth as the signal.	链路提供的内容。码率是要承载的量：发生器的 TS 复用速率（除非你取消勾选）。带宽是频谱上的空间（一个信道、一段频段规划）。C/N 是载波与频谱分析仪上所见噪声之比，在与信号相同的带宽内。
What the mouse wheel, keys and extra mouse buttons do on the spectrum and waterfall: zoom, tune, pan, step size, shortcuts	鼠标滚轮、按键和鼠标额外按键在频谱和瀑布图上的作用：缩放、调谐、平移、步进、快捷键
What the network broadcasts	网络广播的内容
What the public list shows. List or remove the radio with the button under the link at the top.	公共列表显示的内容。用顶部链接下方的按钮列出或移除该电台。
What the setup does	初始设置的作用
What this receiver has actually heard, kept in a local file (30 days, 4 MB) and never uploaded: FT8 / FT4 / WSPR stations with a locator and beacons with a known place. 'Not heard' only means something on bands you were listening to.	此接收机实际收到的内容，保存在本地文件中（30 天，4 MB），绝不上传：带网格定位的 FT8 / FT4 / WSPR 台站和已知位置的信标。「未听到」仅指在你收听的频段上没有听到。

What works, and what would make reception better.	哪些有效，以及什么能让接收更好。
What your CQ says: CQ DX asks for distant stations only, CQ POTA for park hunters ...	你的 CQ 含义：CQ DX 只呼叫远端台站，CQ POTA 面向公园猎手……
What your decodes show: who hears you, antenna pattern, APRS health, talkgroup busy hours, trunk load, automatic pages, overdue broadcasts	你的解码显示的内容：谁听到了你、天线方向图、APRS 健康状况、通话组繁忙时段、集群负载、自动寻呼、逾期广播
Wheel notches per step	每步滚轮格数
When	时间
When a call or track starts and a rule matches, its audio (and optionally a short IQ clip) is saved to your recordings folder. Encrypted or unreadable traffic is never recorded: only the log line is kept. Nothing is ever blocked, and nothing is uploaded. Laws about recording differ by country and by kind of traffic (telephony, pagers, private radio): you are responsible for yours. Files are kept until you delete them.	当呼叫或航迹开始且规则匹配时，其音频（以及可选的短 IQ 片段）会保存到你的录音文件夹。加密或不可读的流量从不录制：只保留日志行。不会拦截任何内容，也不会上传任何内容。关于录音的法律因国家和流量类型（电话、寻呼机、私人电台）而异：你要对自己负责。文件保留到你删除为止。
When a code word is lost, the missing characters are filled in from words this address sent before, but only when one reading clearly beats the others. Guessed characters are shown in colour and listed in the tooltip; characters that cannot be guessed stay as ?.	当一个码字丢失时，缺失的字符会从该地址之前发送的单词中补全，但仅当某一读数明显优于其他读数时。猜测的字符以颜色显示并列在提示中；无法猜测的字符保持为 ?。
When is the band open	频段何时开放
When is the band open##bandopen	频段何时开放##bandopen
When the decoders take longer than this share of real time, the weakest carrier is dropped (never the one you listen to)	当解码器耗时超过实时时间的此比例时，最弱的载波被丢弃（你正在收听的载波从不丢弃）
When the source has another shape than the output (a 4:3 camera in 16:9): stretch it, keep it whole with black bars, or fill the picture and cut the edges	当信号源宽高比与输出不同时（4:3 摄像头放入 16:9）：拉伸、加黑边完整保留，或填满画面并裁掉边缘
When the source has another shape than the output (a 4:3 camera in a 16:9 picture). Stretch: fill the picture, shapes look wider or narrower. Letterbox: keep the whole source, with black bars. Crop: fill the picture and cut the edges.	当信号源宽高比与输出不同时（4:3 摄像头放入 16:9 画面）。拉伸：填满画面，形状看起来变宽或变窄。加黑边：完整保留信号源，两侧黑边。裁剪：填满画面并裁掉边缘。
When this is on, positions decoded from the signals you receive are sent to map.on-air.dev so they can appear on the public map. Catalogue satellite orbits, your receiver location, drone operator locations, privacy-flagged FLARM aircraft, and the built-in test signal are not sent. Your name is not sent unless you choose to type one under Server > Your name on the map; if you do, it is public, shown on the icons you were the last to hear, and searchable. You can turn this off at any time from Server.	开启后，从你接收的信号中解码出的位置会发送到 map.on-air.dev，以便显示在公共地图上。星历目录、你的接收机位置、无人机操作员位置、隐私标记的 FLARM 飞机和内置测试信号不会发送。你的名字不会被发送，除非你在「服务器 > 地图上的名字」下自行填写；填写后将公开显示在你最后收听的图标上并可搜索。你可以随时在「服务器」中关闭。
When this is on, the amateur radio contacts you decode (FT8, FT4, WSPR) and the QSOs you log are sent to map.on-air.dev for the public map: the callsigns of both stations, the mode, the band and frequency, the time, and where each station is as the centre of its locator square (4 characters, about 110 x 55 km, unless you choose 6). Your own station is drawn at your locator square, never at an exact location. Logbook notes, signal reports, your QRZ or other account details and your receiver location are not sent. Callsigns are public information, but this is your choice: it needs "Send data to map server" to be on as well, repeats are sent once, and you can turn it off at any time in the Server menu.	开启后，你解码到的业余无线电通联（FT8、FT4、WSPR）和你记录的 QSO 会发送到 map.on-air.dev 的公共地图：双方的呼号、模式、频段和频率、时间，以及各台站所在网格的中心位置（4 字符，约 110 x 55 公里，除非你选择 6 字符）。你自己的台站绘制在网格中心，绝不绘制在精确位置。日志备注、信号报告、你的 QRZ 或其他账户信息以及你的接收机位置不会发送。呼号是公开信息，但这是你的选择：它还需要「发送数据到地图服务器」同时开启，重复数据只发送一次，你可以随时在「服务器」菜单中关闭。
When this station gets weak, moves to a stronger frequency of the same programme, as a car radio does.	当此台站变弱时，会切换到同一节目的更强频率，就像车载收音机一样。
When you drag the view past the edge of the band, the tuned frequency moves with the radio. Off: only the radio centre moves.	当你把视图拖过频段边缘时，调谐频率随电台移动。关闭：只有电台中心移动。
Where	位置
Where "Hear this channel" plays: the output device, a second output (for example a virtual audio cable for fldigi or WSJT-X) and a local network stream	「收听此频道」的播放位置：输出设备、第二个输出（例如给 fldigi 或 WSJT-X 的虚拟音频线）和局域网流
Where OnAir listens for the RTMP stream, rtmp://address:port/application/stream key. 0.0.0.0 listens on every network card of this PC; port 1935 is the usual RTMP port. In OBS Studio: Settings > Stream, Service "Custom...", and copy the Server and Stream key shown below. One sender at a time; when it stops, OnAir waits for the next one and sends a black picture meanwhile. Windows asks once whether OnAir may accept connections from the network: allow it for private networks.	OnAir 监听 RTMP 流的位置，rtmp://地址：端口/应用/流密钥。0.0.0.0 监听本机所有网卡；端口 1935 是 RTMP 常用端口。在 OBS Studio 中：设置 > 直播，服务选「自定义……」，复制下方显示的服务器和直播密钥。同一时间只有一个发送者；发送停止后，OnAir 等待下一个并同时发送黑画面。Windows 会询问一次是否允许 OnAir 接受网络连接：对专用网络选择允许。
Where a transmitter can be received: predicted SNR on the map from power, frequency, time and the SNR you need. No terrain.	发射机可接收到的范围：根据功率、频率、时间和你所需的 SNR 在地图上预测 SNR。不含地形。
Where are you? This picks the band plan: the TV channels, FM and airband of that country.	你在哪里？这会选取频段规划：该国家的电视频道、FM 和航空频段。
Where are you? This sets your country and radio region, the band plans, the FM step, the TV channels, the aircraft map position and the time zone. You can skip it.	你在哪里？这会设置你的国家和电台区域、频段规划、FM 步进、电视频道、飞机地图位置和时区。你可以跳过。

Where to get it	获取方式
Which channel widths the receiver reads. Auto 1 - 4 MHz runs the radio at 6 Msps (it also looks for the 1 and 2 MHz primary channels of a 4 MHz network beside the centre). Auto, all widths needs 20 Msps (a HackRF, a LimeSDR ...) and reads 1 - 16 MHz.\nOne width sets the radio rate that width needs (2, 4, 5, 10 or 20 Msps) and ignores the other widths.	接收机读取哪些信道宽度。「自动 1 - 4 MHz」让电台运行在 6 Msps（同时查找 4 MHz 网络中心旁的 1 和 2 MHz 主信道）。「自动，所有宽度」需要 20 Msps（HackRF、LimeSDR 等），读取 1 - 16 MHz。\n单一宽度会设置该宽度所需的电台速率（2、4、5、10 或 20 Msps）并忽略其他宽度。
Which streams go into the transport stream. Audio uses part of the capacity. Multiple audio tracks carry the same sound on their own PIDs, to test receivers that offer a language choice.	哪些流进入传输流。音频占用部分容量。多路音轨将相同声音放在各自的 PID 上，用于测试提供语言选择的接收机。
While it applies: the spectrum and telemetry update less often, the window redraws at 30 fps when you type and at 10 fps behind other windows. A playing TV picture keeps its frame rate.	生效期间：频谱和遥测更新频率降低，打字时窗口以 30 fps 重绘，在其他窗口后面以 10 fps 重绘。播放中的电视画面保持其帧率。
While no QSO runs, the band follows the schedule (UTC). "auto" picks the band with the most decodes in the last hour and visits every band now and then.	无 QSO 进行时，频段按时间表（UTC）轮换。「auto」选择最近一小时解码最多的频段，并不时访问每个频段。
While the window is minimised or hidden: the spectrum and telemetry update a few times a second and scans wait. Decoding and audio go on.	窗口最小化或隐藏时：频谱和遥测每秒更新几次，扫描等待。解码和音频继续。
While this mode is on screen and the receiver is stopped. It starts once per window; if you stop it, it stays stopped until the next one. Site times vary.	在此模式显示且接收机停止期间。每个窗口启动一次；如果你停止它，直到下个窗口前保持停止。各地时间不同。

Works out an unknown digital signal step by step: channel, modulation, symbol rate, symbols, line code, frames, checksum and fields. Every stage shows what it found and how sure it is.	逐步解析未知数字信号：信道、调制、符号率、符号、线路码、帧、校验和与字段。每个阶段显示它发现的内容及把握程度。

Workspace	工作区
Wrap the transport stream in RTP packets. Off: plain UDP.	将传输流封装为 RTP 包。关闭：普通 UDP。
Write all these settings to a file, or read them back.	将所有这些设置写入文件，或从文件读回。
Write every message OnAir sends to and receives from SDRconnect (nRSP-ST) with a time stamp to sdrconnect-ws.log in the data folder. For bug reports. IQ data is not logged.	将 OnAir 与 SDRconnect（nRSP-ST）收发的每条消息带时间戳写入数据文件夹中的 sdrconnect-ws.log。用于错误报告。IQ 数据不记录。
Write pcapng files	写入 pcapng 文件
Write the decoded bits, packed into bytes (first bit in the top bit), to a new file in the psk-bits folder of the OnAir data folder	将解码后的比特打包成字节（第一位放在最高位），写入 OnAir 数据文件夹 psk-bits 文件夹中的新文件
Write the log lines of this run to a text file in the data folder.	将本次运行的日志行写入数据文件夹中的文本文件。
Write the stages as name=number, separated by commas. Until then the overall gain alone is used.	将各级写为 name=number，用逗号分隔。在此之前只使用总增益。
